# Runwire 3.0 — unified runtime detailed development plan and tracker

**Target:** Runwire 3.0.0 after published 2.1.2 (commit 7e9bf20ad0737a187dfed77b723791b141b36378); 2.2 is an unpublished historical engineering candidate.
**Location:** infocyph/Runwire only, existing branch plan/runwire-independent-ecosystem-roadmaps and existing draft PR #9. No merge, new branch, tag, release or updates to Foundation, Infbyte, Runwire-Core or Runwire-Cells.
**Snapshot date:** 2026-10-27. **QA-accepted:** **140/169 (82.8%)**; **29 OPEN**. WebTransport excluded. Scoped U10.06 accepts independently verified TLS over cleartext CONNECT and fail-closed rejection of HTTPS-proxy nesting; successful TLS-over-TLS is not claimed. C2 closed after immutable nine-green QA; final release gates are still open.
**Canonical tracker:** 140/169 accepted (82.8%); 29 open; 18 U divisions. C1 and C2 are complete; C3 active (2/9). Historical 175-task and pre-C2 acceptance notes below are archived.
**Decision index:** [Consolidation roadmap](runwire-3.0-unified-runtime-roadmap.md).

## W175 — C3 worker-owned teardown fault isolation (2026-10-27; QA pending)

With PHPForge `dev-main` restored and the prior exact-head H2, hosted, native portable and C3 smoke gates green, harden **partial U12.06**: one periodic timer cancellation fault no longer prevents other timers from cancelling or the worker stop wake and executor drain. Failed timer handles remain available for a retry during terminal `close()`. Worker terminal cleanup always attempts independent coroutine, periodic, network, executor, channel and watcher teardown before rethrowing the first failure; a failed control-watcher cancellation remains retryable. Added a Composer-free fault-injected `.ci/c3_cleanup_fault_smoke.php` gate to the dual-version native C3 workflow. Require positive exact-head PHP8.4/8.5 evidence plus complete QA and all remaining U12 session/fairness acceptance. **No early U12.06 completion: C3 2/9, 140/169 accepted, 29 OPEN.**

## W174 — Restore updated PHPForge main and re-run unwaived CI (2026-10-27; QA pending)

Following maintainer confirmation of an updated PHPForge main, Runwire restores `infocyph/phpforge: dev-main@dev` and removes the temporary fork/commit VCS override from W173. The observed upstream main now specifies ParaTest `>=7.25.0 <7.26.0` and Pest `^5.3.0`; compatibility with secure PHPUnit and current Pest must be resolved by **real Composer CI**, not guessed or waived. Re-run all standard workflows plus PHP 8.4/8.5 native C3 smoke; keep C3 **2/9** and canonical **140/169, 29 OPEN** pending exact-head QA. No changes to upstream PHPForge, security enforcement or other libraries.

## W173 — Runwire-only secure Composer QA dependency bridge (2026-10-27; CI verification pending)

The standard QA and H2/H3 workflows fail Composer resolution before running tests: PHPForge main freezes ParaTest 7.26.0, while current Pest expects ParaTest 7.26.1+ and the available secure PHPUnit conflicts with older Pest. Runwire's development-only `composer.json` now requests the one-commit-ahead PHPForge Dependabot branch (`1f1c7c11f80f`, ParaTest ^7.27.3) through an explicit VCS repository, pinned to that immutable revision, while retaining all PHPForge tooling, four PHPForge QA lanes, both static analyzers and Composer security advisories. This is a **temporary Runwire-only QA bridge**, not an authorized upstream PHPForge update, QA waiver or final releasable dependency contract. Require green exact-head clean installs and nine workflows before any task acceptance. Program **140/169 accepted (29 OPEN); C3 2/9** unchanged.

## W172 — C3 inherited active TCP listener lifecycle guard (2026-10-27; exact-head QA pending)

Runwire-only C3 continuation strengthens `TcpListener` after an active listener is inherited across `pcntl_fork`: active connection abort, graceful shutdown, accept dispatch and connection activation now reject lifecycle operations from the wrong PID. A socket bound but **not started** remains safe to share with forked native workers. Extends the real fork Pest fixture from two to six prohibited operations and adds an independent Composer-free native `.ci/c3_listener_fork_smoke.php` executed on PHP 8.4/8.5 in the C3 workflow. **Partial U12.02 only; no acceptance claimed** until immutable CI, real mixed protocol handoff/ownership and full standard QA pass. C3 stays **2/9**, program **140/169**, **29 OPEN**. Dependency solver remains subject to the unweakened Composer security policy.

## W171 — C3 prefork listener event-loop isolation (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`f16a6ab`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/f16a6aba69878fe2796385c298661f458d1e11dd) preserves legitimate socket binding before `pcntl_fork`, while restricting an *already started* TCP listener to its owning PID on `start`, `close` and accept pause/resume. The real [`b6ab070`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/b6ab070f7efc3d37d0745c062c771af344c8af1b) fork test proves a prebound child can attach its own loop, the parent can subsequently attach its separate loop, and a later inherited *active* listener rejects child-side lifecycle operations. **Partial U12.02; QA pending**. All nine standard QA lanes remain subject to secure PHPForge/ParaTest/Pest/PHPUnit resolution. **C3 2/9 and total 140/169 accepted (29 OPEN) unchanged**; no premature completion.

## W170 — C3 independent native ownership and QA closeout checkpoint (2026-10-27)

[**C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40464286037**](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40464286037) **PASSED both PHP 8.4 and PHP 8.5** on immutable `945e7ed`. It exercises the source syntax of changed executor, worker context, worker groups, pools and dialers; off-reactor file operations; fork-safe executor, QUIC, pool and outgoing connector ownership; worker-owned pending dial cancellation and exact-once cleanup; real HTTP1/TCP/UDP across SIGKILL, recycle, **completed** reload, shutdown; bounded crash-loop restarts; invalid telemetry hysteresis; real autoscale live HTTP traffic with 136/136 correct responses on both PHP versions; and ~23s process/RSS/FD/p99 soak with zero remaining PIDs. These new native gates do **not** silently replace existing PHPForge QA or the independent H2/H3 protocol requirements. `src/Network/Dialer.php`, `HappyEyeballsDialer.php` and worker ownership changes from W169 are independently green.

**C3 code progress:** U11.03 portable-only policy guarded; U12.02 executor/QUIC/pools/dialers fork-safe with intentionally shared prefork listener still requiring explicit full protocol handoff proof; U12.04 live window-driven supervisor worker count verified; U12.05 H1/TCP/UDP verified but H2/H3 mixed-fault acceptance pending; U12.06 owned pools/connections/QUIC/dials/executor shutdown and coroutine registry handling with complete admission/drain proof pending; U12.08 signal/restart-budget verified but concurrent reload/autoscale fault permutations pending; U12.09 23s worker stress verified but complete production-equivalent throughput/fairness certification pending. **No U12 task is falsely marked accepted.**

**External CI blocker:** Six standard PR workflows fail at Composer installation before most tests because `infocyph/phpforge dev-main` restricts ParaTest to 7.26.0 while the supported Pest/modern PHPUnit combination conflicts; pinning available PHPUnit patches hit Composer security advisory `PKSA-qhjm-rpqc-qkn6`. All advisory blocking remains enabled; no outside Runwire repository was edited. Main CI still red, so **C3 2/9, program 140/169 accepted (29 OPEN)** remain authoritative, regardless of native-smoke evidence. No merge, tag or release.

## W169 — C3 outbound dialer ownership, cancellation and native CI (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`c4218cc`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/c4218cc8b6cf6c12aefe5266d07537ffbbcdc0b2) and [`fe38db3`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/fe38db32590aec486ac1f7b71dcf86806e42debc) make numeric-TCP/Unix and Happy Eyeballs dialers PID-bound, so inherited fork children cannot use or close parent-owned pending sockets. Their shutdown loops continue cancelling remaining pending attempts even when a completion observer throws, then preserve the first error. [`b4ff77c`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/b4ff77ccbb1cd9528ec39f414343d371474b22a3) adds explicit worker ownership for pending dial connectors with deterministic shutdown (Happy Eyeballs races first, then underlying sockets). Real fork/pending-socket Pest fixture [`493a257`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/493a257d16ca8fe7f4baf7709d77046a7e357c25), independent native [`1429cce`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/1429cce94ba5a3939382d78e8bf5a5646a08d457), CI [`0b19856`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/0b198560bb642074cbee36376223e5b6ba4f4980) **pending exact-head QA**. Partial U12.02/U12.06: session admission/drain and whole-protocol QA remain open; no blanket acceptance.

## W168 — C3 real HTTP autoscale fairness and portable file worker QA (2026-10-27)

The independently run [C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40463723362](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40463723362) **PASSED on both PHP 8.4/8.5** at immutable [`a7f8e03`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a7f8e03136ea69762629643ab1aae08ba5d9eb20): each lane served **136/136 correct HTTP/1 responses, zero errors, both worker-count directions, no concurrent slot owners, p99 end-to-end local response 0.317 ms (8.4) and 0.327 ms (8.5), and no final worker PIDs** during a 20.5-second load fixture. The diagnostic RPM ~397.7 is fixture-specific, **not a generic application capacity claim**. The independently run [C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40463939584](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40463939584) **PASSED PHP 8.4/8.5** on immutable [`dd090ba`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/dd090ba0aa8196b799740c26df3f5becccb50b76), adding binary off-reactor file write/read/rename/unlink through bounded subprocess IPC plus reactor heartbeat and terminal watcher/timer cleanup. These are targeted positive U11.03/U12.04/U12.09 proofs, **not** final release certification, and full nine-lane security/host/interop quality matrix remains blocked by the PHPForge-Pest-ParaTest/Composer security compatibility fault. **Canonical accepted counts unchanged at 140/169 (29 OPEN), C3 2/9**. No cross-repository work or release.

## W167 — C3 mixed real peers through completed native reload (2026-10-27)

The independent [C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40463494523](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40463494523) is **GREEN on PHP 8.4 and PHP 8.5** at immutable [`592e85f`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/592e85fd5b40435a3de6869739866900de52d978). The new [`824e7d3`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/824e7d3b002389a3168ad328cac90681c3f4f1a2) independent real native peer fixture checks valid HTTP/1, TCP and UDP responses across child SIGKILL/restart, recycle, rolling reload, and final stop; [`592e85f`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/592e85fd5b40435a3de6869739866900de52d978) requires the **completed rolling reload event**, not merely a ready replacement, before success. The dual-version CI also includes live worker autoscale, missing-telemetry hysteresis reset, bounded restart exhaustion, and 23.5 s process/FD/RSS/p99 soak. **Partial U12.05 native H1/TCP/UDP coverage; H2 and H3 real-peer lifecycle acceptance remains OPEN**, as do integrated fairness/admitted-request and full resource-drain proofs. Nine standard PR workflows are still Composer-install blocked by PHPForge's ParaTest/Pest/PHPUnit security-version incompatibility and cannot be marked QA green. **Canonical acceptance unchanged at 140/169, 29 OPEN, C3 2/9.** No outside-Runwire changes.

## W166 — C3 sustained autoscale native two-version QA and telemetry reset (2026-10-27)

The independent [C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40463116876](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40463116876) **PASSED PHP 8.4 and PHP 8.5** on immutable `87cb4c3`, covering source syntax, real scale-in/out, SIGKILL recovery, bounded restart exhaustion, and 23.5 s repeated native scale/worker-process soak. Each PHP lane recorded six worker-count transitions, final child count zero, max parent FD count 12 and peak parent heap 2 MiB; observed native p99 loop timer jitter was **0.235 ms (PHP 8.4)** and **0.145 ms (PHP 8.5)**, child RSS peaks approximately 18.6 MB and 18.9 MB respectively. The benchmark is host-specific diagnostic data; it is **not** an end-to-end request-RPM claim. Soak code [`ff55493`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/ff554932adcbae88ed67c8dcd36f8b03c7a14246), CI [`87cb4c3`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/87cb4c31338b44ac634dbf4c5d6db7bd9a3d7055). Subsequent [`10e08f2`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/10e08f28fbbad16bcebb182ce1071a2fce9bec4e) and [`2fa5fd5`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/2fa5fd526d9fc5403626d77a5cee898bb0249c4a) invalidate previous hysteresis votes when telemetry is missing/malformed; Pest regression [`0b44a31`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/0b44a31461b97d09e10af13ad1433b821109086f), independent native contract [`9ab992e`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/9ab992e0477b0190d6e89625b7bca9eb418b1e17), CI [`d1f93de`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/d1f93de2baf282192303011af741dfcb4ec5528b) are **pending exact-source QA**. Even prior native green is partial evidence: U12.04/.08/.09 require complete transport-fairness and full immutable QA before task acceptance. The PHPForge/ParaTest/PHPUnit secure Composer resolver blocker persists in the nine established workflows. **Canonical totals unchanged: 140/169 accepted, 29 OPEN; C3 2/9**. No unrelated repository changes.

## W165 — C3 native QA branch, live autoscale proof and crash-loop stress (2026-10-27)

The dependency-free [C3 Native Supervisor Smoke #40462814810](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40462814810) **PASSED both PHP 8.4 and PHP 8.5** at immutable `caacc6b` after the first red test correctly exposed insufficient stable scaling windows. [`0d205b9`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/0d205b91da6cb60f2b3b66a5a783aa325f56b3f2) prevents scale-out from overlapping a draining predecessor in the same slot and restores deferred slots once the previous owner is reaped; [`f39857c`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/f39857c5e6d899fa444785c0fa99519c7847407e) checks real worker-slot uniqueness. [`875aac0`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/875aac0163053b6ff91013e04544a97da2007f20) and [`716254a`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/716254ab10e687327203959c18a3508f21ff634c) retain a safe unchanged worker count on malformed telemetry. [`8b904e8`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/8b904e8d60390ea0d277cd060fc3e8f7e98a642f) and CI [`bf9c9dd`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/bf9c9dd4af13d869b34cec3d80a50abad5fe31c4) provide live two-PHP-version source syntax and independent real native worker scaling acceptance, **without disabling Composer security**. [`9fe4c66`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/9fe4c66ed5eb066316eb5d7493159745f42f4379) and CI [`d4c56d0`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/d4c56d0574a544ab9fd9f4e800492136b3226893) extend the native gate to SIGKILL restart and crash-budget fault injection; those checks are **pending**. Full immutable nine-green existing workflows and all U12.04/.08 acceptance remain outstanding; existing PHPForge clean-install dependency conflict still blocks them. **U12.04 and U12.08 remain OPEN**, canonical C3 2/9, overall 140/169 (29 OPEN) unchanged.

## W164 — C3 U12.04 live autoscale native slot lifecycle (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`1bcc87d`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/1bcc87d177e8734cfb06158fb0c316993c676b51) adds immutable worker group count copies, and [`4853a94`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4853a94873b82377a2aedbd91a43def240eaba41) connects the existing `AutoscaleWindowController` to the real supervisor loop: callers configure a bounded per-group policy plus explicit metrics sampler before startup; sustained sample windows scale out by forking real slots, and scale in by canceling pending restarts and graceful signaling excess workers. It avoids scale decisions during a rolling reload and cancels sampling on supervisor stop. [`f3977eb`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/f3977eb1d9186af625410741e8159988802c5b87) adds multi-window real child-process scale-in/out test. **U12.04 remains OPEN** until immutable CI, no-oscillation and admitted-request preservation proofs; competing reload/restart and exception/metrics fault paths require further QA. This is actual scaling implementation, not a decision-only stub. Dependency blocker W158 remains unresolved and security policy must not be disabled. Canonical **140/169 accepted, C3 2/9, 29 OPEN**.

## W163 — C3 active worker-owned TCP teardown (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`aaa4a9f`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/aaa4a9f48c1098cbade42db62242673b060fda0a) adds explicit active `Connection` ownership to the worker context. Existing pool close retires queued waiters and idle sockets, but intentionally leaves active leases to their owners; the new opt-in `ownConnection` closes those active accepted connections on drain/stop without claiming pool ownership automatically transfers outstanding leases. [`8eb07e3`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/8eb07e3f548a68357c782ae37bdb2995be3bb705) adds live loopback TCP proof for active socket retirement, idempotent stop, and zero terminal loop watchers. **This is partial U12.06, not closure**: full cross-transport session ownership and exact-head green QA remain mandatory. C3 2/9, overall 140/169 (29 OPEN) unchanged; current PHPForge Composer install blocker remains. No Foundation/Infbyte/PHPForge changes, merge, tag or release.

## W162 — C3 owned network resource teardown (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`c78c83b`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/c78c83bfc0bd697b5c485039c0293c6bb500da10) adds explicit opt-in worker ownership of bounded connection pools and native QUIC client sessions and closes them during both stop/drain and final close, independently of worker-owned file executors. The [`053d0b2`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/053d0b2842a2b89f38b0d2df0d55e5d92771ea1a) regression covers pending connection-pool waiter cancellation, repeated stop and timer/watcher retirement. **Partial U12.06 only; QA pending.** Outstanding are full pending outbound dial cancellation, protocol session ownership, listener handoff, supervisor autoscaling and fault/soak tests. Clean-install CI remains blocked by PHPForge `brianium/paratest` pin and Composer security resolution; no disablement of audit. Canonical **140/169 accepted, 29 OPEN, C3 2/9** unchanged.

## W160 — C3 fork-state snapshot isolation (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`890e870`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/890e870fcff728a28c6a03c1298d340b1dfc616e) and [`98af154`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/98af154fc1c408a776df25213c24ebdf9d77d35e) extend process ownership assertions to pool active/idle/waiter counters and optional metrics. This prevents a forked child interpreting an inherited parent's counters as its own live capacity. [`819d448`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/819d448ccbb82eb46bccdf4a71e592fee07a6d87) extends the real fork test to cover eleven prohibited inherited operations while leaving parent resource accounting functional. This is **partial U12.02** only; listener handoff, live scaling, full QA and secure dependency resolution remain open. Accepted totals **unchanged at 140/169, C3 2/9, 29 OPEN**.

## W159 — C3 U12.02 fork-safe pools and native QUIC facade (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`d2a593b`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/d2a593bf115390e487fe7fc08200d5163a2ff5b6) and [`f251ef7`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/f251ef70f504d19eee4a8a3a22021dcc0ecfbfde) add fail-closed PID ownership to bounded active connection pool acquire/cancel/release/close and idle verified connection pool store/take/close. The real [`bbb1a11`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/bbb1a110ba820a6e546be54b2a069ce805b0bbde) fork test proves a child cannot reuse/retire a parent's pool, while the original parent can still acquire/release. [`538b85c`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/538b85c0b3047f84191539a0172c42f6ba5e3fb9) adds native QUIC facade process identity checks and [`298e2cf`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/298e2cf2cf09e7aa30722fa33e76b4bfbcac10a5) adds live cross-fork refusal tests. Existing intended prefork listener socket sharing must not be forbidden without explicit listener owner transfer; this remains a separate U12.02 acceptance gap alongside full cleanup/QA. **U12.02 not promoted.** Program still 140/169, 29 OPEN; C3 2/9. All new source requires immutable PHPForge/native/live-interop QA, currently installation-blocked by the W158 advisory.

## W158 — C3 QA installation security blocker (2026-10-27; unresolved)

The temporary Runwire-only compatibility pin at `ec9c586` was **reverted** by [`a77ec31`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a77ec31e99a42d31f5589edc7a4424011b9d9870) after [H2 job #122042288673](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40460072073) proved Composer rejects PHPUnit 13.4.1–13.4.5 due to published security advisory `PKSA-qhjm-rpqc-qkn6`, not merely dependency incompatibility. Disabling Composer advisory enforcement or forcing a vulnerable package is **not permitted**. Original PHPForge dev-main demands ParaTest >=7.26.0 <7.26.1, while current Pest requires a newer ParaTest; clean installs cannot currently certify new C3 commits. Preserve all red install evidence; require a secure PHPForge/Pest/ParaTest/PHPUnit version set before QA acceptance. No out-of-repository modifications, no fake green QA, **C3 remains 2/9, program 140/169, 29 OPEN**. The earlier W157 pin is historical and reversed.

## W157 — CI dependency resolution repair, Runwire-only (2026-10-27; QA pending)

The exact `459b78f` CI source failed real hosted and independent H2 matrix **before tests** at Composer install because `infocyph/phpforge dev-main` requires `brianium/paratest >=7.26.0 <7.26.1`, while Pest 5.3.2/5.3.3 demand ParaTest ^7.26.1, and a newer PHPUnit patch conflicts with available older Pest. Both failure suites show Composer solver errors, not assertions or runtime regressions. [`ec9c586`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/ec9c5868d05883af90a128e3199aa465041d8e7c) pins only Runwire **dev** dependencies Pest 5.3.1 and PHPUnit 13.4.1, matching the PHPForge-constrained ParaTest 7.26.0 lineage. This is a temporary compatible CI solver hypothesis requiring confirmation on all QA lanes; it neither weakens tests nor edits PHPForge or other repositories. Unpin once PHPForge supports the newer ParaTest/Pest combination. **C3 stays 2/9, 140/169 accepted (29 OPEN)** until full exact-head QA. No 3.0 release or merge authorization.

## W156 — C3 U12.06 shutdown callback-failure isolation (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`4bbcb89`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4bbcb89443455e519d181d0fbe90e3b956560c37) ensures `ProcessExecutor::close()` retires remaining workers, queued jobs and drain watchers even when application completion observers throw; the first exception is preserved after cleanup. [`df4e1b5`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/df4e1b5dbb76d2b186c1c3cb2fbbca8e870ae0a6) isolates worker-owned executor close exceptions so one failed observer cannot prevent shutdown of other owned executors. [`11e3b8e`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/11e3b8e02fa2846405d93ed55ee19c0943e22b6e) proves two separate executors release their jobs and watcher resources despite a throwing completion observer. **Partial U12.06 only**: pending exact-head green QA and ownership/drain proof for non-executor resource classes. C3 remains 2/9, program 140/169 (29 OPEN); no release.

## W155 — C3 process-fork drain-observer hardening (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`c54098c`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/c54098c1dc31cccbf996e614b1412c06338d61df) extends the executor PID ownership guard to `onDrained` subscriptions (not just submit/cancel/close); [`080fd22`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/080fd2244150196d4d7896932b7cd15541ad9b13) extends the live `pcntl_fork` regression to four rejected child-side operations, then validates parent-side work remains functional. This strengthens **partial U12.02**. Full listener/pool/QUIC fork-safety proof, exact-source native/PHPForge QA, and whole C3 completion remain open. **No change** to 140/169 QA-accepted, 29 open, C3 2/9.

## W154 — C3 U12.02 cross-fork executor ownership guard (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`defac448`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/defac4483cfb38b5f5be6151b2645e65ddb6cd6f) binds process-backed executor submit/cancel/close to the creating PID, refusing inherited child use or child-side termination of a parent's worker process. [`9b516b9`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/9b516b9b4df4bef455e0fb7b9fad293e8406deee) adds an actual `pcntl_fork` regression checking child-side refusal and continued correct parent executor work. This addresses only **one U12.02 resource class**: listener, outbound pool, native QUIC, loop reactor ownership and complete supervisor fork-cleanup proof still require implementation/real-peer acceptance. **U12.02 remains OPEN; U12.06 remains OPEN; U11.03 remains QA pending.** No new acceptance beyond 140/169 (82.8%), 29 OPEN, C3 2/9. Full exact-head PHPForge/interop QA is mandatory.

## W153 — C3 U12.06 worker-owned executor drain (2026-10-27; QA pending)

[`6940706`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/6940706022c49ea53ce91e19f035877dd1499606) adds explicit opt-in process-executor ownership to native `WorkerContext`: stopping/closing a worker requests cancellation and shutdown of registered owned process executors, whereas borrowed executors are unaffected. [`3fdc3a3`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3fdc3a3c0e3353ef6dbbec9efd8e7e51e578283b) and follow-up [`3d742fa`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3d742faf634ba870c45c5b8ef710488e46ed2ab9) add the real queued/active job cancellation and watcher cleanup test. No tracker checkbox is promoted on unverified new source.

## W149 — C2 scope freeze, WebTransport removal audit and QA handoff (2026-10-27)

**Maintainer-scoped C2 exit:** Continue to preserve ordinary HTTP/3, native QUIC streams, independent UDP and WebSocket. All WebTransport-specific public protocol/server/client implementation, design pages, task IDs and catalog references are excluded; `tests/ReleaseScopeExclusionTest.php` scans every PHP file under `src/` to prevent accidental reintroduction. Native `QuicClientOptions` RFC 9221 DATAGRAM capability refusal is **retained** because it is independently used to protect plain QUIC consumers and fixtures, not because WebTransport is in scope. The current source scope is **169 canonical tasks**, not the historical 175.

**U10.06 scoped boundary (NOT a claim of functional TLS-over-TLS):** The supported positive case is independent origin certificate/SNI/ALPN verification over an already-negotiated *cleartext* CONNECT tunnel, checked against a separate Python TLS origin. Wrong identity is rejected. If the proxy socket is already encrypted (HTTPS proxy), an attempted second TLS upgrade **aborts the proxy socket and throws**, without invoking successful origin callbacks or downgrading identity; an active callback owner is not stolen. This unsupported HTTPS-proxy→HTTPS-origin mode is excluded from the 3.0 supported API/acceptance target and documented in `docs/features.rst`, `docs/connection-policies-3.0.rst` and `docs/migration-3.0.rst`. This C2 scope change is explicit, **not positive nested TLS implementation**.

**Immutable implementation under verification:** [`7d71b589`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/7d71b589acfb9e2fa6b88c6513e08dd22c1fad2c) has successfully passed Source Audit, Sphinx, independent H2, Release Candidate, Portable Native, Real Hosted, Swoole and Security & Standards; adaptive H1/H2 Benchmarks was in progress at the handoff-writing checkpoint. All nine routine exact-source workflows are mandatory before marking U10.06 QA accepted and C2 5/5. Existing U10.03/04/05/09 have immutable independent QA receipts. After green CI, record the final accepted source and move C2 to DONE before starting C3; do not assert C5/final-release certification.

**Version choice:** WebTransport's removal alone does not make a minor release safe. The published 2.1.2 → planned 3.0 public manifest looks largely additive/optional, but the source audit explicitly does not prove behavioral compatibility. Keep the 3.0 target until U16.12/U17 migration, signature, ownership and host behavior audits freeze the actual API; use a 2.x minor only if those audits establish fully backward-compatible behavior. No release, tag or merge authorized.

## W148 — C2 U10.06 encrypted-proxy fail-closed upgrade guard (2026-10-27)

**Implemented (QA PENDING; not task accepted):** [`4072006e`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4072006ec4bce4d076d15913d5436d56223f6f4f) hardens `Internal/VerifiedTargetTlsUpgrade::assertAllowed`: an encrypted proxy transport is **aborted before rejecting** the attempted nested TLS upgrade, so it cannot remain open and be mistakenly reused as an origin-verified connection. Also refuses PHP stream resources with existing native TLS crypto metadata even if the connection's encrypted flag was incorrectly set. Tests in `tests/VerifiedTargetTlsUpgradeGuardTest.php` verify that unsupported nested TLS closes the source and does not invoke completion, that claimed/active tunnel ownership is rejected without stealing the owner, and that watchdog/FD watchers are not leaked. This is defensive protection, **not real nested TLS support**. Verified original-target TLS through a **cleartext** CONNECT proxy remains the supported independently tested path from the prior [`26ce43df`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/26ce43dfde4785a2303b324bee2d18529c17673d) implementation.

**C2 QA gate:** exact-head full PHP8.4/8.5 PHPForge/analysis, native QUIC, independent HTTP/2 and benchmarks must complete; the new guard alone cannot accept U10.06. No downgrade of proxy or origin TLS identity is allowed. **Tracker unchanged: 138/169 QA-accepted, 31 OPEN; C2 4/5, U10.06 OPEN.** No WebTransport reintroduction.

## W147 — Approved Runwire 3.0 protocol exclusion and rebaseline (2026-10-27)

**Current approved scope:** WebTransport is intentionally **removed entirely from Runwire 3.0**, not partially implemented and not a mandatory release gate. The selected PHP native QUIC backend (ext-quic/OpenSSL) does not expose the RFC 9221 QUIC DATAGRAM capability required for the originally scoped implementation. This is **not** a restriction on ordinary QUIC, HTTP/3, WebSocket or UDP datagrams. No fake datagram emulation, unused client/server sessions or public protocol API is shipped. The superseded `docs/webtransport-design-3.0.rst` and its Sphinx index/catalog entries were deleted; see `docs/features.rst` (Release status interpretation and limitations) and `docs/transport-guide-3.0.rst` for the clear release note.

**Canonical rebaseline, not fake task completion:** Retire U09.05 (accepted architecture-only task) and U09.06–U09.10 (five never accepted). Original 175 becomes **169 in-scope canonical tasks**, accepted 139 becomes **138**, remaining 36 becomes **31**, so **138/169 (81.7%)**; U09 becomes **4/4 complete**. Rebaseline C1–C5 from 52 to **47 assigned tasks**, 16 accepted and 31 open. C2 now covers exactly U10.03, U10.04, U10.05, U10.06, U10.09: **4/5 accepted and 1 OPEN (U10.06)**. U14.04 is retained as a WebSocket-only hostile-frame/cancellation requirement. U16.06 remains accepted for HTTP/1.1/2/3 and WebSocket documentation, not for the removed architecture design. Existing native QUIC DATAGRAM capability checks continue failing closed rather than promising a feature.

**Still mandatory:** U10.06's original-target identity and unsupported nested TLS require their existing security decision; C3–C5 and full immutable-source release certification remain OPEN. Rebaseline `release-certification.yml`, associated preflight/tests and both plan trackers to **169**. Historical W-prefixed notes below retain prior source observations only; they are superseded wherever they describe excluded work or old 175-task totals. No merge/tag/release.

- **One active closure batch only:** **C1 8/11**, three open: `U07.06` (independent H3 control/QPACK ownership and server interoperability), `U07.07` (concurrent sibling H3 blocking/unblocking, cancel/reset correctness), `U07.11` (matched real native H1/H2/H3 outbound RPS, p99, CPU and RSS). All three tasks must pass their full independent acceptance evidence and exact-head QA before C1 is DONE. **C2 1/10, C3 1/9, C4 0/13 are PAUSED; C5 0/9 QUEUED**. Earlier authorization for parallel development is superseded by the latest finish-one-complete-batch-first direction.
- **Release boundary:** no merge, tag, release or cross-repository edits. Final 2.1.2→3.0 matched saturation, 1800-second soak, hosted Read the Docs preview and C5 certification are **not** implied by the routine green checks. This tracker-only commit itself must receive its own exact-source CI before any new QA acceptance.

## Development rules

- One established-connection engine for incoming and outgoing streams. Connection attempts, client/server TLS roles, UDP/QUIC and protocol state machines remain separate where behavior demands it.
- Public API breaking changes are allowed for a coherent architecture, but every changed API needs a migration guide and tests.
- No hidden blocking: DNS and filesystem use genuine non-event-loop execution or explicitly reject unsupported modes; keep optional native extensions optional.
- Full 3.0 scope includes networking (TCP/TLS/Unix/UDP/QUIC), async DNS, dialing, pooling, proxies, relay, HTTP1/2/3 and WebSocket clients/servers, filesystem, Fiber concurrency, autoscaling, all hosted integrations, security, observability and complete docs.
- 64-bit PHP 8.4+ and Composer remain the baseline. No new dependency or release gate on unrelated Infocyph repositories.
- A closure batch is accepted only after every assigned U task and complete required exact-source green QA. **C1 and C2 have completed their whole-batch QA; C3 is the active closure batch. C4/C5 remain paused until C3 acceptance.** Historical C1-only execution notes below describe an already-completed prerequisite. Intermediate commits are not sub-batches. Update both trackers and PR together on each push. Only independently QA-proven tasks count as accepted.
- Keep 2.1.2 as immutable baseline. Final matched throughput requires five 180-second pairs, warmup, successful RPS, both CV below 2.5%, strict p99/RSS/correctness budgets, generator headroom, and 1800-second process-tree soak. A passing mock or microbenchmark cannot replace real protocol QA.
- **Sphinx documentation is mandatory:** use **exclusively native reStructuredText (.rst)** for all published documentation pages, with an InterMix-style `docs/conf.py`, `docs/index.rst`, Read the Docs configuration v2, and no MyST or `.md` Sphinx pages. Convert legacy end-user Markdown guides to RST before release. Internal trackers/evidence are outside the published Sphinx source graph. Require, complete detailed feature list, generated PHP API reference, migration guides and strict docs CI. All U16 tasks are required for 3.0.
- **Task proof convention:** change [ ] to [x] only after Acceptance is verified and update Proof from PENDING to immutable commit, real test and Actions/artifact link. No plan-only task completion.

## Complete-the-entire-batch QA workflow (mandatory)

**An execution batch is the complete set of assigned canonical U tasks, not a commit, a task group, or a QA correction.** C1 and C2 are complete; C3 is active, with exact-head whole-batch QA still required before C4. Each retains its separate whole-batch acceptance. All 11 task acceptance criteria are required in full, including real independent TLS/QUIC/HTTP peers, rollback/cancel/flow controls, matched benchmarks and performance evidence. Routine QA alone does not close C1.

1. Implement every remaining C1 task within Runwire and the existing PR #9. Intermediate commits are allowed to obtain evidence, but they **are not additional batches**.
2. Diagnose every red check; require green PHPForge PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest, static analyzers, native QUIC, host, benchmarks and every C1-specific independent gate on the exact final source. Do not bypass or soften acceptance.
3. Use the canonical U task checklist to record proof and immutable Actions links. Count an accepted item only after every stated criterion is met. A pending, canceled, skipped or red job cannot prove acceptance.
4. Keep both plan and roadmap synchronized with every push; retain accurate status and do not advance completion percentages on feature scaffolding.
5. Only after C1 is 11/11 accepted with all required exact-head QA green mark C1 DONE; then activate C2 and require its own independent full QA. Apply the same whole-batch completion rule to C2, C3, C4 and C5.
6. Stay on the existing Runwire branch and draft PR #9. No merge, tag, release, new branch, work in Foundation/Infbyte/Core/Cells, or publishing.

### Cross-batch delivery and QA discipline

- **C1:** start with capability/test boundaries and essential infrastructure defects (frozen release checkout, fail-closed certification aggregation, exact-source evidence collection). Work next through QUIC transport, H3 client and protocol pressure. Track implemented-versus-accepted separately.
- **C2:** finish the scoped proxy/original-target TLS identity and pooling work without authentication downgrades. WebSocket/HTTP3/QUIC retain their existing supported protocols; excluded protocol support is not an implementation gate.
- **C3:** connect bounded autoscale *decisions* to real native supervisor lifecycle and its crash/drain QA, prove filesystem acceleration/performance and adversarial session limits.
- **C4:** run genuine matching and resource telemetry once real protocol capabilities exist; perform the complete RST migration/hosted docs preview while code stabilizes.
- **C5:** certify an immutable final head. No U17 checkbox and no release-ready claim is permitted without its task-specific proof, including U15 performance, U16 hosted preview, independently verified peers and full PHP 8.4/8.5 gates.
- **Whole-batch gate:** all eleven C1 tasks must be individually accepted and exact-source CI plus independent H3 peer/matched benchmark evidence GREEN before C1 DONE. **C1 and C2 already closed; do not promote C3 until exact-head full QA is green.** Intermediate commits remain within C1, not sub-batches. Keep both trackers and PR in sync.
- **Evidence/preflight distinction:** opt-in immutable-source certification must be runnable to *produce* U15/U17 evidence before all tasks are checked; the authoritative final aggregation **still refuses** release readiness unless exactly 169/169 tasks and every required job succeed. Never downgrade a failed/skipped gate to green.

[source a5a900b](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a5a900b701ef67035218402249ec66a689e4d520); [PHPForge #39913487501](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39913487501) QA/analysis relevant lanes PASS..

Real 100ms subprocess work permits concurrent loop timer progression in `tests/ProcessExecutorTest.php`; [benchmarks #39913487092](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39913487092) executor profile passed PHP8.4/8.5; [source a5a900b](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a5a900b701ef67035218402249ec66a689e4d520)..

[PHPForge QA #39913487501](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39913487501) four test matrices PASS; [source a5a900b](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a5a900b701ef67035218402249ec66a689e4d520)..

[Implementation 4cc06dd](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4cc06dda9eddc03cde57a715cd311d355ba80d98), [Security & Standards #39921145750](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39921145750) all 4 PHPForge QA lanes and both analyzers PASS.
