- [x] **U02.03** Define OPEN, HALF_CLOSED, DRAINING and CLOSED state contracts. **Acceptance:** Forbidden transition and double-close tests. **Proof:** `ConnectionState::HALF_CLOSED`, `Connection::shutdownWrite()` and internal `ConnectionLifecycleOperationsTrait` provide OPEN→HALF_CLOSED→CLOSED local write-side shutdown, preserve reads, forbid duplicate half-close or writes after shutdown, and idempotently close. `tests/ConnectionStateOwnershipTest.php` runs a real Unix stream pair, verifies post-half-close inbound data, pause/resume and zero remaining watchers. [Source 1ac186f](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/1ac186f6b2b14c2dfbf1c70290c7509750d5ea68); [all four PHPForge QA lanes and both analyzers #39923215737](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923215737) PASS.
- [x] **U02.04** Unify buffered send/receive, watermarks and partial-write handling. **Acceptance:** Bounded memory under slow peers. **Proof:** The same `Network\\Connection` and `Network\\Internal\\ByteQueue`/`ByteBudget` serve accepted and verified dialed streams, with symmetric bounded receive/send, distinct high/low watermarks, immediate/partial write paths and rejection on budget overflow. `tests/ConnectionTest.php` and `tests/AsyncConnectionTest.php` exercise slow-reader pressure, partial writes, recovery and exact buffer limits. [PHPForge #39923215737](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923215737) all four test lanes PASS; [two core benchmark jobs #39923214991](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923214991) PASS on PHP8.4/8.5.
- [x] **U02.05** Define callback-to-Fiber ownership transfer and read watchers. **Acceptance:** No duplicate frames or stale callbacks. **Proof:** `Connection::claimCallbacks()` and `handoffCallbacks()` share exclusive `ConnectionCallbackOwnership`; handoff now replays existing buffered bytes to the new protocol owner once, checks terminal closure and refuses ownership release by non-owner. Real stream `tests/ConnectionStateOwnershipTest.php` proves split buffered bytes transfer without duplication/stale callback and zero retained watcher. [Source 1ac186f](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/1ac186f6b2b14c2dfbf1c70290c7509750d5ea68); [QA/analysis #39923215737](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923215737) PASS.
- [x] **U02.06** Attach deadlines, timers and connection cleanup to owner scope. **Acceptance:** Cancellation closes exactly once. **Proof:** `src/Network/Internal/ConnectionScopeOwnership.php` and `Connection::bindOwner()` enforce single ownership, earlier inherited/explicit monotonic deadline, cancellation close-once, unsubscribe and timer cleanup. `tests/ConnectionScopeOwnershipTest.php` exercises active and already-cancelled owner, both deadline priority directions and watcher/timer/subscription cleanup. [Implementation and regression a356384](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a35638472361995396980e6aa1065326bed92ccd); [QA #39926291446](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926291446) all PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest Pest/PHPCS/Pint/Rector and both analyzers PASS.
- [x] **U02.07** Integrate platform socket options with explicit policy overrides. **Acceptance:** Correct TCP tuning and precedence. **Proof:** `ConnectionFactory::accepted()` and `dialed()` now apply explicit TCP_NODELAY consistently before handoff; `TcpSocketTuner` preserves explicit overrides over protocol defaults and reports unsupported native options instead of silently ignoring. `tests/ConnectionSocketPolicyTest.php` exercises actual TCP sockets in both directions for on/off precedence plus Unix exclusion. [Implementation 54c30e0](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/54c30e0fbd95f0e5f1fc97db15b523db49d48925); [QA/analyzers #39926291446](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926291446) all six gates PASS.
- [x] **U02.08** Preserve inbound hot paths and raw/line/length-prefix framed behavior. **Acceptance:** Existing server tests and benchmarks pass. **Proof:** `TcpListener` and `UnixListener` now adopt streams through the shared factory without duplicating established send/receive/watchers. Existing `tests/TcpListenerTest.php`, `tests/UnixListenerTest.php`, `tests/FramedConnectionTest.php`, `tests/Http1ConnectionTest.php` and protocol integration tests pass in all four [PHPForge suites #39923215737](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923215737). [Hosted runtime acceptance #39923214954](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923214954) and both [core benchmark jobs #39923214991](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39923214991) PASS; the extended adaptive H2 tail is tracked separately.
- [x] **U02.09** Exercise many simultaneous opens/closes, EOF and worker shutdown. **Acceptance:** No FD, timer or buffer leak. **Proof:** `tests/ConnectionChurnTest.php` tests 128 simultaneous real Unix stream pairs with mixed peer EOF and 16 owner cancellations, zero retained watchers, subscriptions, reserved bytes and leaked `/proc/self/fd` handles; a second real 24-client TCP listener shutdown test verifies accepts, close-once and no live connections/watchers. [Implementation a356384](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a35638472361995396980e6aa1065326bed92ccd); [QA/analyzers #39926291446](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926291446) all six gates PASS; [Portable Native #39926290615](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926290615) and [Hosted #39926290763](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926290763) PASS. Full 3.0 production-process-tree soak remains U15/U17.

**Batch tracker:** 9/9 accepted; 0 open — **U02 COMPLETE**. New factory, real metadata, half-close, symmetrical byte bounds, ownership handoff, timer/cancellation teardown, socket option precedence and 128-socket/24-listener churn have individual proof above. All four PHPForge QA and both analyzers PASSED at source `a356384` ([run #39926291446](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39926291446)); source audit, Sphinx, release candidate, portable/hosted runtime and Swoole PASSED. The extended H3/adaptive H2 diagnostic tails in Security & Standards/Benchmarks were still active when this acceptance was written; do not claim **eight workflows green** until their final conclusions are verified. **Next batch:** U03.

### U03 — Async DNS and outbound TCP/Unix dialer

**Prerequisite:** U01,U02. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U03.01** Define Dialer and DialOptions public endpoint and error contracts. **Acceptance:** Typed URI and invalid-address tests. **Proof:** `src/Network/DialEndpoint.php` and `DialOptions.php` define validated immutable TCP/Unix endpoint and deadline/socket contracts without DNS I/O; `tests/DialEndpointTest.php` covers IPv4, bracketed IPv6, hostnames, Unix paths and invalid/credential/NUL/timeout forms. [Source 50fe962](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/50fe962257b2de6050b22a6f004dfb41faaf4e69); [four QA lanes and both analyzers #39929786108](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39929786108) PASS.
- [x] **U03.02** Implement nonblocking TCP connect with write readiness and error verification. **Acceptance:** Real IPv4/IPv6 TCP peer acceptance. **Proof:** `src/Network/Dialer.php` launches nonblocking numeric TCP connects with `STREAM_CLIENT_ASYNC_CONNECT`, watches writable readiness and verifies `SO_ERROR` before factory handoff, rejecting synchronous hostname DNS. `tests/DialerTest.php` checks a real IPv4 loopback accepted peer, and a bracketed IPv6 loopback peer when the host supports it; absent host IPv6 capability is explicitly detected. [Source 50fe962](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/50fe962257b2de6050b22a6f004dfb41faaf4e69); [QA/analysis #39929786108](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39929786108) four PHPForge and both analyzers PASS. No real DNS or address race is claimed.
- [x] **U03.03** Implement Unix-domain outgoing connections. **Acceptance:** Real Unix peers and permission errors verified. **Proof:** The same `Dialer` and `ConnectionFactory::dialed()` support nonblocking Unix-domain outgoing streams, with direct endpoint parsing, real peer acceptance and missing-socket error tests in `tests/DialerTest.php`. [Source 50fe962](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/50fe962257b2de6050b22a6f004dfb41faaf4e69); [all QA/analyzer jobs #39929786108](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39929786108) PASS; watchers, timers and pending sockets return to zero.
- [x] **U03.04** Implement genuinely off-loop A/AAAA DNS with bounded work. **Acceptance:** DNS delay cannot stall event-loop heartbeat. **Proof:** `src/Network/AsyncDnsResolver.php` delegates A/AAAA lookups to the existing bounded real `ProcessExecutor` worker IPC without blocking the loop; `tools/executor-worker.php` now falls back to OS hosts-based IPv4 resolution if DNS records are unavailable (e.g., localhost), deduplicates valid addresses and never runs worker I/O in the parent loop. `tests/AsyncDnsResolverTest.php` verifies DNS behind a 60ms blocking-worker fixture while a 3ms loop heartbeat progresses, exact-once cancellation, no dangling watchers or subscriptions. [Source 3232cfd](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3232cfde3683d16849381a1085d141e5cd1561e5); [Security & Standards #39967719050](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39967719050) four PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge suites and both analyzers passed.
- [x] **U03.05** Implement Happy Eyeballs address selection and staggered racing. **Acceptance:** Slow/failing address fallback without leaked sockets. **Proof:** `HappyEyeballsDialer` and `Internal/HappyEyeballsAttempt` stagger and interleave validated IPv4/IPv6 candidates over the single `Dialer`, cancelling unselected attempts on winner handoff; `tests/HappyEyeballsDialerTest.php` verifies live failing IPv6 → successful IPv4 fallback and DNS-to-dialer hostname connection without leaked watchers. [Source 3942b0e](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3942b0e276cce6883689a88a29a98482aa8e42b7); [all PHPForge QA and analyzers #39975849650](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39975849650) PASS.
- [x] **U03.06** Support per-stage and overall deadlines, cancellation and cleanup. **Acceptance:** Late DNS/connect events ignored. **Proof:** An overall monotonic deadline and per-candidate remaining time control DNS resolution and staggered sockets; cancellations invalidate DNS jobs, timers and losing sockets before late callbacks. `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` tests already-queued DNS cancellation, 15ms deadline behind a 80ms worker and no late completion or handles. [Source 3942b0e](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3942b0e276cce6883689a88a29a98482aa8e42b7); [PHPForge four QA lanes and both analyzers #39975849650](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39975849650) PASS.
- [x] **U03.07** Limit concurrent pending dials and queued endpoints per scope/worker. **Acceptance:** Dial storm bounded RSS/FD. **Proof:** `HappyEyeballsDialer` bounds host races per instance, the shared `Dialer` caps pending sockets, `ProcessExecutor` caps worker/queue jobs, and DNS/address candidate lists are limited to sixteen. `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` verifies rejection at two pending host races and 17 candidate addresses, exactly-once teardown and zero watcher leaks. [Source 3942b0e](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3942b0e276cce6883689a88a29a98482aa8e42b7); [four PHPForge QA lanes and both analyzers #39975849650](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39975849650) PASS. Broader sustained FD/RSS certification remains U15.
- [x] **U03.08** Integrate with Fiber scope and callback style without duplication. **Acceptance:** Direct and coroutine clients use same connector. **Proof:** `Coroutine\\Network\\AsyncDialer` adapts the same shared callback-style Happy Eyeballs and established `Connection` to a scope-owned Future and `AsyncConnection`, inheriting the scope cancellation token and retaining single ownership. `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` exercises real TCP Fiber-await handoff through `CoroutineRuntime` and clean closure; direct callback tests exercise the same connector. [Source 3942b0e](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3942b0e276cce6883689a88a29a98482aa8e42b7); [PHPForge QA and static analyzers #39975849650](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39975849650) PASS.
- [x] **U03.09** Validate Swoole/OpenSwoole reactor support and no blocking fallback. **Acceptance:** Both host modes pass or report clear limits. **Proof:** `tests/fixtures/swoole_coroutine_server.php` now exercises `AsyncDialer` resolving localhost off-loop through the host-owned `SwooleLoop` without nested event loops. `.github/workflows/swoole-coroutine.yml` verifies the `/dial` acceptance response on Swoole and OpenSwoole under PHP 8.4 and 8.5. [Source c11e1b1](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/c11e1b122f49f93cdba98ef0dfe804b3275f209e); [four hosted extension/PHP jobs #39976909971](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39976909971) PASS.
- [x] **U03.10** Benchmark successful overlap and contention versus sequential connects. **Acceptance:** Throughput, p99, CPU, RSS, errors recorded. **Proof:** `benchmarks/u03_dial_profile.php` records real loopback sequential versus overlapping verified TCP connects, successful connects/s, p99 ms, parent CPU, Linux `/proc/self/status` RSS, failures, accepted peer counts and zero remaining watchers/timers/sockets. The existing [Benchmarks #39977854249](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39977854249) U03 comparison passed on PHP 8.4 and 8.5 and archived per-PHP JSON. [Four PHPForge QA lanes and both analyzers #39982999768](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39982999768) passed at the integrated U04 source head. This is a single-machine diagnostic, not U15 release-duration matched certification.

**Batch tracker:** 10/10 accepted; 0 open — typed endpoint/option contracts, real nonblocking numeric TCP connect and Unix-domain dialing passed all PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA and both analyzers on source `50fe962` ([run #39929786108](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39929786108)). IPv6 real-peer test is host-capability conditional. **Completed:** U03.10 real sequential-vs-overlapped dial benchmark [passed on both PHP versions in #39977854249](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39977854249), with Linux RSS, CPU, p99, throughput, error and resource cleanup evidence. No U03 tasks remain open. U03.09 Swoole/OpenSwoole hosted compatibility now PASSED all 4 PHP/extension combinations in [run #39976909971](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39976909971). U03.10 source and preliminary benchmark step exist; final quality gates still pending. U03.04 off-loop DNS is accepted via [QA #39967719050](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39967719050); direct hostname dialing awaits these later gates. Extended H3/adaptive H2 diagnostic jobs are separate from these focused QA results. **Exit:** all U03 evidence linked, full required QA green, no unresolved critical regression.

### U04 — Unified TLS security and client/server handshakes

**Prerequisite:** U02,U03. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U04.01** Separate client verification policy from server identity configuration. **Acceptance:** Secure peer/hostname checking defaults. **Proof:** `src/Network/TlsClientOptions.php` enforces chain and hostname verification, server name indication, trust-root settings, TLS1.2/1.3-only methods and no insecure downgrade independently of inbound `TlsOptions`. `tests/TlsClientOptionsTest.php` and `tests/TlsClientDialerTest.php` exercise secure defaults, invalid policies and verified localhost TLS. [Source 84feb5a](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/84feb5ae46b2cee7ced3c2d0882717e414850391); [four QA + two analyzer jobs #39982999768](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39982999768) PASS.
- [x] **U04.02** Implement genuinely asynchronous client TLS handshake. **Acceptance:** TLS1.2/1.3 read/write readiness proven. **Proof:** `Internal/ClientTlsHandshake.php` starts TLS over an already-connected nonblocking TCP socket with read/write readiness and finite cancellation/timeout handles before passing an encrypted stream via the *same* `Dialer`/`ConnectionFactory` and Fiber `AsyncDialer`. `tests/TlsClientDialerTest.php` verifies separate forced TLS 1.2 and 1.3 server negotiations, correct ALPN, actual loopback endpoints, silent-TCP deadline failure and watcher cleanup. [Source 84feb5a](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/84feb5ae46b2cee7ced3c2d0882717e414850391); [PHPForge QA/analyzers #39982999768](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39982999768) PASS.
- [x] **U04.03** Share correct TLS state machinery between inbound and outbound. **Acceptance:** Existing server ALPN tests preserved. **Proof:** `Internal/TlsHandshakeReadiness.php` owns deadline, readable and optional writable watches for both `TlsHandshake` inbound and `ClientTlsHandshake` outbound. Exact-once `close()` frees all watches and timers on success, failure and cancellation; `tests/TlsHandshakeReadinessTest.php` covers cancellation and server deadline, and the real server/client TLS ALPN tests remain green. [Source 4663567](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4663567331ea2ec2af693d81fc63c72887dc8b36); [full PHP 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge and both analyzers #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462) PASS.
- [x] **U04.04** Implement SNI, ALPN, trust roots and hostname/SAN checks. **Acceptance:** Wrong/untrusted peer rejected. **Proof:** Outbound TLS options supply explicit peer_name/SNI_server_name, native OpenSSL verify_peer and verify_peer_name (including hostname/SAN when supplied by the peer), ALPN and optional CA file/path; peer checking cannot be disabled in this policy. Real `TlsClientDialerTest.php` verifies an explicitly trusted peer succeeds, wrong host and untrusted self-signed peer are rejected, and h2 ALPN negotiates for TLS 1.2 and 1.3. [Source 84feb5a](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/84feb5ae46b2cee7ced3c2d0882717e414850391); [PHPForge #39982999768](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39982999768) PASS. Dedicated SAN-only fixture coverage belongs to the later expanded security/fuzzing gates.
- [x] **U04.05** Support mutual TLS identities with bounded chain validation. **Acceptance:** Positive and negative mTLS tests. **Proof:** `TlsClientOptions` exposes optional outbound client certificate/private-key/passphrase without disabling trust/hostname verification; `TlsOptions` on the server enforces trusted CA configuration and verifies a presented client peer certificate. Real loopback positive (trusted client identity) and negative (missing client identity), policy rejections and server trust-depth tests are in `tests/TlsClientDialerTest.php`, `tests/TlsClientOptionsTest.php`, `tests/TlsListenerTest.php`. [Source bc837b8](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/bc837b88aaf10a5e32e84da7e62c269da0954a73); [PHP 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest QA and both analyzers #39989750473](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39989750473) PASS.
- [x] **U04.06** Implement handshake timeout, cancel, close-notify and truncation handling. **Acceptance:** Zero orphan TLS watchers or insecure downgrade. **Proof:** `Connection` now rejects raw encrypted TCP half-close (which cannot send TLS close_notify) and classifies outbound TLS EOF without an exposed authenticated notify state as `TLS_UNVERIFIED_EOF`, never a normal application EOF. Existing graceful `fclose` shutdown uses OpenSSL shutdown; `tests/TlsUnverifiedEofTest.php` verifies retained delivered bytes, no false EOF callback, terminal error and zero orphan watchers, while real TLS timeout/mTLS and no-downgrade paths remain green. [Source c25fd06](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/c25fd0681e98fd95962f061d37bf7fa09577b4ab); [four PHPForge QA and two analyzers #39995711371](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39995711371) and all eight source workflows PASS. **Limitation:** PHP streams do not reveal trustworthy peer close_notify state: a legitimate TLS peer EOF may be conservatively rejected. See `docs/plans/evidence/runwire-3.0/u04-tls-eof-policy.rst`. Full interoperability/replay robustness remains U14/U17.
- [x] **U04.07** Maintain optional OpenSSL and explicit TLS unavailable failure. **Acceptance:** Plain TCP still usable without ext-openssl. **Proof:** Plain `Connection` and TCP sockets remain available without an OpenSSL extension, while client `TlsClientOptions::context()` throws an explicit unavailable error, never downgrades and is covered by `tests/fixtures/no_openssl_outbound.php` under `php -n` in the `Portable Native` workflow. [Source b0edf1d](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/b0edf1d334ba98f3bf97a288bc3a35fdde3f3cf7); [PHP 8.4/8.5 portable-native #39989750415](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39989750415) PASS, [four PHPForge QA and two analyzers #39989750473](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39989750473) PASS.
- [x] **U04.08** Profile TLS latency and steady-state RPS/RSS versus 2.1.2. **Acceptance:** Matched result within strict performance limits. **Proof:** [C1 exact-source five-pair TLS acceptance #40202656645](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40202656645) with [immutable artifact #12603187659](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40202656645/artifacts/12603187659): published 2.1.2 `7e9bf20` against `d923713` (5×180s each, same PHP8.4 runner, physical-core pinning, 30s warmups and verified TLS); baseline/candidate successful RPS 390.374/390.362, p99 41.12/41.14ms, peak RSS 120197120/127311872 bytes, both RPS CV 0.009%; comparison reports passed=true and budget_enforced=true; all nine regular d923 source QA workflows passed. This accepts this TLS-specific baseline gate, not U15, H3 full certification or release.

**Batch tracker:** 8/8 accepted; 0 open. Secure outbound verification policy, nonblocking client TLS 1.2/1.3 and SNI/ALPN/native chain+hostname checking verified by real loopback tests and [all four PHPForge QA lanes and both analyzers #39982999768](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39982999768). **Accepted:** U04.08 matched verified-TLS performance comparison [C1 acceptance #40202656645](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40202656645); U04.06 fail-closed unverified TLS EOF and encrypted close semantics accepted by [QA #39995711371](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39995711371), with the documented PHP peer-close-notify observability limitation. U04.03 shared nonblocking TLS readiness/deadline owner passed [all six task-specific QA/analyzer lanes #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462). Positive/negative server-client mTLS and no-OpenSSL failure are accepted by [four QA lanes and both analyzers #39989750473](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39989750473). **Exit:** all evidence linked, full required QA green, no unresolved critical regression.

### U05 — UDP, outbound QUIC and unified datagram transport

**Prerequisite:** U02,U03,U04. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U05.01** Unify inbound and outbound UDP ownership, message limits and metadata. **Acceptance:** IPv4/IPv6 datagram interoperability. **Proof:** `DatagramClient` delegates to the same `DatagramListener` bounded UDP watcher, payload/metadata and send engine for connected/unconnected ownership, with verified numeric-only endpoints and no synchronous hostname fallback. `tests/DatagramClientTest.php` verifies real IPv4 and IPv6 (kernel-capability conditional) reply round trips, correct peer metadata and zero watchers on closure. [Source 20e7f42](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/20e7f42ffa95e63b02e93c84bcea0f43dfc81af3); [full PHP 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge and both analyzers #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462) PASS.
- [x] **U05.02** Implement connected and unconnected UDP client operations. **Acceptance:** Bounded send/receive and deadline tests. **Proof:** Connected client `send()` and unconnected `sendTo()` both use the established UDP nonblocking engine, with bounded `DatagramOptions`. One-shot `DatagramClient::receiveOnce()` registers a finite event-loop deadline and cancels its timer/read watch exactly once on a reply or timeout. `tests/DatagramClientTest.php` verifies real response, silent-peer deadline and leak-free cleanup. [Source 20e7f42](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/20e7f42ffa95e63b02e93c84bcea0f43dfc81af3); [full PHP 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge and both analyzers #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462) PASS.
- [x] **U05.03** Model UDP truncation, zero-length messages and packet-size ceilings. **Acceptance:** Exact datagram boundary semantics. **Proof:** `DatagramListener` reads at `maxDatagramBytes+1`, rejects truncated packets and never combines message boundaries; `DatagramOptions` enforces a 65,507-byte ceiling and `DatagramWriteResult` rejects oversize sends. `tests/DatagramClientTest.php` proves zero-length datagrams delivered, oversize packets dropped, following packet delivered intact, and exact counts. [Source 20e7f42](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/20e7f42ffa95e63b02e93c84bcea0f43dfc81af3); [full PHP 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge and both analyzers #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462) PASS.
- [x] **U05.04** Implement outbound QUIC client connection and TLS validation. **Acceptance:** Independent QUIC server acceptance. **Proof:** src/Network/QuicClientSession.php and tests/fixtures/quic_session_echo_client.php prove verified TLS 1.3, ALPN and same-worker outbound connection against independent aioquic, Security & Standards [#40064041388](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064041388) at source 80120be (four PHPForge QA lanes, both analyzers and native H3/QUIC PASS).
- [x] **U05.05** Support QUIC unidirectional/bidirectional streams and flow-control. **Acceptance:** Real reset/cancellation and sibling isolation tests. **Proof:** [exact-head source `aa09a568`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/aa09a568a2da225ff8deba166f21525353189c58), [Security & Standards #40212536140](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212536140) (both PHP 8.4/8.5 native QUIC jobs, four stable/lowest PHPForge QA, both analysis lanes) and [Benchmarks #40212535821](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212535821), plus seven other required regular source workflows, all green; independent aioquic two >32-KiB concurrent bidirectional echoes, client/server initiated uni streams with peer FIN proof, 24 real native stream resets in four waves, two recovered sibling echoes, bounded worker admission/partial writes/FIN/EOF and stalled-peer sibling liveness; [PHP 8.5 QUIC session artifact #12606590142](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212536140/artifacts/12606590142) and [PHP 8.4 artifact #12606292276](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212536140/artifacts/12606292276). Flow control is provided by the verified ext-quic backend; this does not assert RFC 9221 datagrams or H3 multiplex readiness.
- [x] **U05.06** Add negotiated QUIC datagram support and capability checks. **Acceptance:** Unsupported backend fails explicitly. **Proof:** Native ext-quic explicitly advertises no RFC9221 datagrams and refuses requireDatagrams/native datagram dispatch both before connection and while verified, independent aioquic runtime negative fixture and [Security & Standards #40064842606](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064842606) at source 3a9d9a7 PASS. This is capability rejection, not claimed working datagram transport.
- [x] **U05.07** Define QUIC path/migration/rebinding/connection-ID policy. **Acceptance:** Independent migration or deliberate rejection verified. **Proof:** QuicClientOptions pins the established host/port and rejects alternate paths and allowMigration before off-loop connect; ext-quic does not advertise connection-ID migration. Explicit deliberate rejection is exercised with independent aioquic fixture and source-linked [#40064842606](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064842606), not a successful network migration claim.
- [x] **U05.08** Retain application 0-RTT disabled without proven replay policy. **Acceptance:** No unexpected early application dispatch. **Proof:** QuicClientOptions refuses allowEarlyData, assertApplicationDispatchAllowed(false) rejects unverified 0-RTT, and QuicClientSession rejects commands until TLS/ALPN handshake is verified. Positive session plus negative real-peer checks in [#40064842606](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064842606), immutable source 3a9d9a7.
- [x] **U05.09** Fault-test loss, reorder, cancellation storm and stalled peers. **Acceptance:** Safe bounded resources and protocol state. **Proof:** [exact-head source `aa09a568`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/aa09a568a2da225ff8deba166f21525353189c58) with [Security & Standards #40212536140](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212536140) and [Benchmarks #40212535821](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212535821), all nine default workflows green: native PHP8.5 aioquic impaired-UDP interop enforces observed post-handshake drops and delayed/reordered deliveries by packet counters; same-session 24-reset storm, two post-reset healthy siblings and uni-FIN peer receipt; real stalled-peer stream isolated from a healthy sibling before and after reset; native PHP8.4/8.5 QUIC interop passes; limited workers/timers/readers and exact completion checked; [PHP8.5 peer artifact #12606590142](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40212536140/artifacts/12606590142). This is QUIC resilience certification for U05.09, not an end-to-end saturation/soak or full H3 benchmark.
- [x] **U05.10** Benchmark real QUIC poll/write budget and H3 transport costs. **Acceptance:** p99, RPS, CPU, RSS and packet metrics. **Proof:** immutable [source a4a2d6b](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a4a2d6b26836cf89f4cfc3830077266b7803439c); [Security & Standards #40215640638](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40215640638) with both native QUIC PHP8.4/8.5, all four PHPForge QA and both analyzers; [Benchmarks #40215640344](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40215640344) with both PHP versions and the remaining seven default source workflows GREEN. Independent aioquic verified 16 serial h3 requests with zero errors, source-linked successful RPS, p50/p95/p99, parent CPU and peak PHP RSS in archived native H3 profile; real long-lived QUIC worker emitted measured 38 native poll calls / 1,392,236 ns, 5 writes / 362,521 ns, 33 reads / 212,981 ns, 79,123 transmitted bytes, 79,104 received bytes, 4.83 ms worker CPU and 33,420 KiB peak RSS (PHP8.5); independent impaired peer exports positive actual post-handshake UDP datagram counts, drops and delayed/reordered packets to CI artifacts. This accepts the U05.10 *development workload cost profile* only; NOT saturated H3 production performance, matched multi-protocol U07.11/U08.09 or release U15/U17.

**Batch tracker:** 10/10 accepted; 0 open — U05.10 accepted on measured native poll/write + independent H3/profile/packet evidence from #40215640638 and #40215640344; U05.05/.09 accepted on exact-head #40212536140 independent native peer/fault/sibling verification; U05.06/.07/.08 explicit ext-quic unsupported datagram, migration and 0-RTT safety policies passed real negative+positive aioquic [#40064842606](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064842606); U05.04 independent native QUIC TLS/ALPN connected-session acceptance at [QA #40064041388](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40064041388). Previously verified shared bounded connected/unconnected UDP, IPv4/IPv6 real-peer tests, exact zero-length/oversize packet boundaries and one-shot deadline cleanup all passed [PHPForge four QA lanes and both analyzers #39992267462](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39992267462). **Remaining:** No canonical U05 tasks; final release-wide performance gates remain separately open in U15/U17. **Exit:** all U05 evidence linked, full required QA green, no unresolved critical regression.

### U06 — Structured concurrency, tasks, deadlines and scopes

**Prerequisite:** U01,U02,U03. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U06.01** Unify Fiber tasks and network I/O under one owner scope. **Acceptance:** Pending dials cancelled at scope shutdown. **Proof:** `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` parent-failure child dial cancellation, pending-race and zero-watcher assertions; [PHPForge QA and PHPStan/Psalm #40050545361](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545361), [Swoole/OpenSwoole #40050545190](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545190).
- [x] **U06.02** Propagate parent and child deadlines to DNS/TCP/TLS/protocol/files. **Acceptance:** No child outlives forbidden deadline. **Proof:** `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` queued DNS, nested TLS handshake, parent scope failure; `tests/AsyncConnectionTest.php` socket receive deadline; `tests/ProcessExecutorTest.php` off-loop file deadline; `tests/BatchLCoroutineSchedulerTest.php` attached protocol request deadline and child cleanup; PHPForge QA [#40055732464](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732464), Swoole/OpenSwoole [#40055732298](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732298).
- [x] **U06.03** Implement explicit transfer of pooled or worker-owned resources. **Acceptance:** No accidental request-to-request retention. **Proof:** `tests/AsyncConnectionTest.php` twelve live pool adoption/handoff cycles, failed adoption, plus `tests/ConnectionScopeOwnershipTest.php` repeated-owner resource cleanup; [PHPForge QA and PHPStan/Psalm #40050545361](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545361), [Swoole/OpenSwoole #40050545190](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545190).
- [x] **U06.04** Resolve task completion versus cancellation races exactly once. **Acceptance:** No double-future completion or late callback. **Proof:** `tests/CoroutineHardeningTest.php` 64 cancellation/future settlement races and `tests/BatchLCoroutineSchedulerTest.php` observer isolation; [PHPForge QA and PHPStan/Psalm #40050545361](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545361), [Swoole/OpenSwoole #40050545190](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545190).
- [x] **U06.05** Preserve TaskGroup, Channel, Mutex, Barrier and TaskLocal semantics. **Acceptance:** Existing concurrency correctness tests retained. **Proof:** `tests/CoroutineHardeningTest.php` task-local/channel/mutex/barrier cooperation, plus existing `tests/BatchMStructuredConcurrencyTest.php` TaskGroup coverage; [PHPForge QA and PHPStan/Psalm #40050545361](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545361), [Swoole/OpenSwoole #40050545190](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40050545190).
- [x] **U06.06** Bound scope tasks, waiters and queued network/filesystem operations. **Acceptance:** Hard quota and overload policy. **Proof:** `tests/CoroutineHardeningTest.php` task and primitive admission; `tests/AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` bounded DNS/hostname races; `tests/ProcessExecutorTest.php` bounded worker + filesystem queue and 512-cycle FIFO tombstone reclamation; PHPForge QA [#40053268784](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40053268784), coroutine host QA [#40053268453](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40053268453).
- [x] **U06.07** Support callback and coroutine paths through identical connection primitives. **Acceptance:** No duplicate I/O state machine. **Proof:** `src/Coroutine/Network/AsyncConnection.php` adapts established `Connection`; `tests/AsyncConnectionTest.php` callback/Fiber transition, scoped disposal vs explicit transfer, 12-cycle same-socket re-adoption, stale observer reclamation; `tests/ConnectionScopeOwnershipTest.php`; PHPForge QA [#40053268784](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40053268784), Swoole/OpenSwoole [#40053268453](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40053268453).
- [x] **U06.08** Run live mixed coroutine, socket and timer endurance tests. **Acceptance:** Bounded scheduler lag and no FD/RSS leak. **Proof:** `tests/AsyncConnectionTest.php` 2048 real socket/Fiber/timer generations, 32 Linux VmRSS/FD snapshots, p99 latency and scheduler lag bounds, exact stream cleanup; PHPForge QA [#40055732464](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732464), native coroutine-host QA [#40055732298](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732298). Final matched production release performance remains independently required under U15/U17.

**Rolling batch U06-A (2026-10-24; pending QA):** Fixed `AsyncConnection::dispose()` detaching scope shutdown ownership: disposing the adapter must release its callback observer without orphaning the underlying connection when the scope finishes; explicit `transferTo()` alone may hand ownership to a pool. A real socket-pair regression verifies normal scope-exit abort, zero callback/watch leaks, and no premature close at disposal. A separate bounded `ProcessExecutor` admission test rejects the third job at worker+queue capacity while previously admitted sleep and filesystem read finish intact, with no worker/watch leaks. Targets U06.07 and U06.06. No additional task is accepted until the new exact commit passes the full task-specific QA and remaining criteria.

**Rolling QA correction for U06-A (2026-10-24):** On [PHPForge #40052752620](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40052752620), Pest exposed one obsolete test asserting `AsyncConnection::dispose()` leaves an established stream OPEN after its owning scope completes. This conflicts with the verified resource-lifetime guarantee and would permit orphan sockets. The existing test now asserts OPEN while its callback adapter is detached inside the active scope and CLOSED with LOCAL_ABORT once the scope exits; adapter registration is still available to direct core callbacks. The new scope-shutdown regression remains. PHPStan and the standard core benchmarks passed on U06-A; fresh complete QA on the combined B commit is required before accepting U06.06/U06.07. No acceptance counter changed.

**Rolling batch U06-B (2026-10-24; QA pending):** Added an idle real socket receive nested inside an 80ms child deadline, asserting cancelled suspension, owned stream abort and zero orphan watchers/timers/tasks. The mixed timer/socket endurance corpus now runs 128 sequential request generations instead of 48, samples per-request latency and requires measured p99 below 750ms, less than 16MiB allocator growth, Linux FD drift no more than four, plus a clean loop after each request. Targets U06.02 and U06.08; these remain unaccepted until green CI and any remaining protocol/sustained release certification evidence.

**Rolling batch U06-C (2026-10-24; pending QA):** The off-loop `ProcessExecutor` now compacts canceled queued IDs after a bounded threshold without changing live FIFO submission order or admitting jobs above maxWorkers+maxQueue. Stale queue tombstones were previously able to accumulate even when logical `diagnostics()['queued']` stayed bounded. The queue compaction is isolated in `ExecutorPendingQueueTrait` to keep `ProcessExecutor` within the PHPStan cognitive-complexity budget. A regression performs 512 admit/cancel cycles at a one-worker/one-queue cap, checks physical queue bound and exactly-once callbacks, and confirms zero leftover handles after shutdown. Targets U06.06. U06.06 stays OPEN until exact-head full QA proves no regression, then the batch tracker and roadmap must be updated simultaneously.

**Batch tracker:** 8/8 accepted; 0 open. **Exit:** all evidence linked, required QA green, no unresolved critical regression.

- [x] **U07.03** Implement safe H1 connection reuse and streaming upload/download. **Acceptance:** No response bleed or unbounded buffering. **Proof:** H1 sequential reuse without response bleed, 128-KiB SHA-correct streamed upload and chunked response, bounded 8-KiB socket queue, and independent dialed TCP peer: tests/Http1PoolReuseIntegrationTest.php, Http1StreamingUploadTest.php, Http1OutboundClientTest.php; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA and both analyzers PASSED on immutable source 3426f3c [#40103162591](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40103162591). H2/H3, active pooling and retries not included.
- [x] **U07.04** Implement HTTP/2 client preface, SETTINGS and HPACK. **Acceptance:** Independent H2 reference-server conformance. **Proof:** exact `493ee5892be57` [hyper-h2 TLS/ALPN/HPACK #40144460404](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40144460404) PASS in PHP8.4/8.5, all four PHPForge Pest/Pint/PHPCS/Rector QA lanes plus both analysis lanes PASS in [Security #40144460808](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40144460808), [Source Audit #40144460434](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40144460434) PASS. Acceptance is **unreleased H2 multiplex only**, not pooling, H3 or final U17 soak/performance certification.
- [x] **U07.06** Implement HTTP/3 client control streams, QPACK and QUIC ownership. **Acceptance:** Independent aioquic/ngtcp2 server conformance. **Proof:** Immutable [source d5c6436c55](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/d5c6436c55fce4d4fa1cad624fc677761b6c5232) and [Security & Standards #40334382707](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40334382707) native PHP 8.4/8.5 + four PHPForge QA + two analyzers PASS; verified H3 control/critical stream ownership, peer SETTINGS and QPACK bidirectional instruction streams, native wire stream IDs, aioquic independent outbound H3 request/response/trailers, malformed peer registry tests and ngtcp2 native interoperability.

Explicit application-level cross-version retry orchestration is **not** claimed; fallback policy is fail-closed.

PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest Pest/Pint/PHPCS/Rector/Deptrac and analyzers PASS at [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793), [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104). No ambient cookie jar, H2/H3 client or unsafe upload replay promised.

[Security & Standards #40168119891](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40168119891), [Source Audit #40168119704](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40168119704) and [independent H2 #40168119708](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40168119708) PASS. **Profile quantified only; no demonstrated improvement or frozen-release performance certification** (two integer CV observations exceed 2.5%). U15 and U17 remain OPEN.

Real independent aioquic H3 dynamic QPACK blocking pressure includes >0 delayed headers, successful RPS/p99/CPU/RSS; separate native QUIC worker accepts opt-in measured poll/write budgets and independent impaired UDP packet evidence. This accepts U08.05 as a *development source+independent runtime profile*, not final matched release certification or H3 outbound dynamic-QPACK client U07.06.

New client-only budgets remain independently QA-gated.

### U09 — WebSocket client/server

**Prerequisite:** U05,U07,U08. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U09.01** Share WebSocket framing, masking, control frames and close semantics. **Acceptance:** RFC6455 fuzz and interoperability. **Proof:** One shared `WebSocketFrameParser` and `WebSocketWireCodec` now enforce RFC6455 client/server masking direction, reserved opcode/RSV rejection, minimal 7/16/64-bit payload encodings, control/close/UTF-8 rules and full shared-worker byte reclamation. `tests/WebSocketAdversarialFrameTest.php` exercises binary payloads at 0/125/126/127/65535/65536 boundaries with 97-byte fragments, invalid bitfields, close codes and role masking; `tests/WebSocketClientRoleTest.php`, `tests/WebSocketFrameParserTest.php` and real socket-peer `tests/WebSocketIntegrationTest.php` verify client/server roles and H1 upgrade/fragment/ping/close interoperability. Exactly source [a0812bc](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/a0812bc12da4445b244aa44ba6d87c78fe01191e), [Security #40076825500](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40076825500): four PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA jobs and both PHPStan/Psalm jobs PASS. No WebTransport/client handshake or large-scale performance certification claimed.
- [x] **U09.02** Implement outbound WebSocket client with TLS and subprotocols. **Acceptance:** Independent server connects and closes safely. **Proof:** Independent WebSocket HTTP1 peer and verified WSS TLS/hostname/ALPN/subprotocol/close handshake: tests/WebSocketOutboundClientTest.php, WebSocketOutboundTlsTest.php, WebSocketClientHandshakeTest.php; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA and both analyzers PASSED at exact source 3426f3c [#40103162591](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40103162591). No H2/H3 extended CONNECT or WT claimed.
- [x] **U09.03** Unify WS heartbeat, fragmentation, backpressure and message limits. **Acceptance:** High connection counts bounded. **Proof:** 128 concurrent fragmented WS peers, independent heartbeat/pong ownership, bounded slow-peer queues and close-time resource retirement proven by tests/WebSocketConcurrentSessionsTest.php, WebSocketBackpressureAcceptanceTest.php, WebSocketAdversarialFrameTest.php. Four PHPForge QA lanes, both analyzers and real native/portable/Swoole tests PASS [Security #40104271461](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40104271461) at source 49b0e9d. WebTransport scaling remains open.
- [x] **U09.04** Validate H2/H3 extended CONNECT only if implemented and qualified. **Acceptance:** No unsupported version advertised. **Proof:** Shared H2/H3 RequestHeaderValidator rejects :protocol pseudo-header for websocket/webtransport while retaining ordinary CONNECT authority. tests/ExtendedConnectRejectionTest.php covers both versions and variants. All PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest Pest/PHPForge and PHPStan/Psalm passed [Security #40073762272](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40073762272) at 8ea14d1; unsupported H2/H3 extended CONNECT not advertised.

**Batch tracker:** 4/4 accepted; 0 open — Accepted: U09.01, U09.02, U09.03, U09.04. All in-scope WebSocket tasks passed their recorded QA; excluded WebTransport work has been removed from this checklist.

### U10 — Connection pooling, proxy negotiation and stream relay

**Prerequisite:** U03,U04,U06,U07. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U10.01** Define pool isolation keys by endpoint, TLS, ALPN, proxy and scope. **Acceptance:** No cross-origin/tenant reuse. **Proof:** New final readonly `Network\\PoolIsolationKey` derives a collision-resistant tuple digest from validated TCP/Unix endpoint host/port/path, tenant/worker scope, TLS peer and caller-rotated trust/client identity, negotiated ALPN, proxy endpoint and proxy authentication policy revision. Missing or mismatched TLS/proxy/ALPN context is rejected; `tests/PoolIsolationKeyTest.php` verifies origin, port, scope, Unix transport, trust-policy, ALPN and proxy-auth separation. The E6 redundant null-safe correction is reflected in exact source c2ce077; all four PHP8.4/8.5 Pest/Pint/PHPCS lanes and both PHPStan/Psalm analysis jobs pass [Security #40071567489](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40071567489). This accepts key isolation **only**, not live pooled reuse/U10.02.
- [x] **U10.02** Implement bounded pool, wait queue, timeout, eviction and health checks. **Acceptance:** No infinite waiter or stale socket. **Proof:** src/Network/BoundedConnectionPool.php limits active leases/dial permits, FIFO cancelable timed waiters and admission overload, reuses only verified healthy scoped IdleConnectionPool sockets with TTL eviction; tests/BoundedConnectionPoolTest.php verifies live socket reborrow, stale EOF, cross-tenant separation and no infinite waiter; tests/BoundedConnectionPoolStressTest.php runs 8 quota-saturated waves of 4 active/32 waiters, bounded memory and FD count. All PHP8.4/8.5 QA and both analyzers pass [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104) at [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793). Dialing and H2/H3 multiplex slot management explicitly remain separate and unimplemented.
- [x] **U10.03** Integrate HTTP/2 and HTTP/3 multiplex slots and GOAWAY draining. **Acceptance:** Correct concurrent request capacity. **Proof:** Accepted at immutable [`ebdd60fe`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/ebdd60feb81b83f1fc661802761f9e969d90e06e), all nine exact-source PR workflows green: [Security & Standards #40353244848](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244848) (PHP8.4/8.5 native QUIC/H3, four PHPForge QA combinations, PHPStan/Psalm), [Benchmarks #40353244007](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244007), [independent H2 #40353244214](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244214), [portable native #40353243954](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353243954), [real hosted #40353244015](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244015), [Swoole #40353243969](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353243969), [source audit #40353244090](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244090), [RST #40353244008](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244008), [release candidate #40353244298](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40353244298). Real independently negotiated H2 two-live-stream test with peer GOAWAY, per-peer SETTINGS capacity, cancellation/tombstones, H3 four simultaneous aioquic stream fixtures and independent native QUIC impairment under PHP8.4/8.5; `Http3ClientAdmissionGate`, `Http3ClientGoawayState`, `Http3ClientControl`, `Http2ClientMultiplexSession`, and `Http2MultiplexConnection` ensure quotas, FIFO, GOAWAY refusal/preservation and no sibling abort. Native QUIC stream budget reserves root/critical handles and worker idle poll advances retransmission. Does not claim connection-pool hit rate (U10.09), full HTTP CONNECT TLS (U10.04/U10.06) or WebTransport (U09).
- [x] **U10.04** Implement HTTP CONNECT proxy tunneling and authentication. **Acceptance:** Real proxied TLS interoperates. **Proof:** Immutable [508d8706](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/508d8706480dd0e0430fa52a8c6d320fa2ecdec4) accepted full independent Python TLS-authenticated CONNECT proxy to a separately bound real TCP origin and returned live application bytes; incorrect credentials received 407 and never contacted origin. Implemented through live PHP Dialer, verified TLS proxy name, bounded HttpConnectTunnel and strict Basic handshake, with cancellation, timeout and cleanup tests. See tests/HttpConnectIndependentRelayTest.php and tests/fixtures/independent_http_connect_relay.py. Exact source nine workflows GREEN: [Security #40355819271](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355819271) (native H3 PHP8.4/8.5, all PHPForge stable/lowest, static analyzers), [Benchmarks #40355818582](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818582), [H2 #40355817943](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355817943), [Hosted #40355818562](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818562), [Portable #40355818032](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818032), [Swoole #40355818096](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818096), [Audit #40355818371](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818371), [Docs #40355818342](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818342), [RC #40355818002](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40355818002). This accepts verified TLS to the proxy and real CONNECT forwarding only; original-target nested TLS/SNI/ALPN is U10.06 and remains OPEN.
- [x] **U10.05** Implement SOCKS5 TCP negotiation and remote/local DNS policy. **Acceptance:** Auth and DNS leak protections tested. **Proof:** bounded RFC 1928/1929 greeting, password and CONNECT bytes; verified outbound proxy TLS name required for credentials; local numeric IPv4 mode blocks unresolved host before any greeting; remote hostname ATYP=3 transmitted only on explicit opt-in; live authenticated TLS negative password/method downgrade, wrong proxy identity and DNS-leak fixtures in [620730b](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/620730bf295e9cd85a9ad187943a252667f015ef). Separate Python-process TLS RFC1928/1929 reference validates authenticated proxy, target IPv4/domain, port and downgrade in [cc1ec90](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/cc1ec9011da10ec492d2e6e07932f1a92a2e43ea). [Security & Standards #40271565881](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40271565881) PHP8.4/8.5 Pest/Pint/PHPCS+analyzers and all nine exact-head routine CI GREEN. **Authenticated proxy negotiation is not nested original-destination TLS**; U10.04/U10.06 remain OPEN.
- [x] **U10.06** Verify original-endpoint TLS on supported cleartext CONNECT tunnels and explicitly reject unsupported TLS-over-TLS through HTTPS proxies. **Acceptance:** Independently verify origin certificate/hostname, SNI, ALPN and application bytes; refuse mismatched identity; abort any encrypted proxy attempted as an origin-verification substitute; no proxy-induced downgrade or resource leak. **Proof:** QA-accepted on immutable nine-green implementation source [`7d71b589`](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/7d71b589acfb9e2fa6b88c6513e08dd22c1fad2c), independent origin TLS reference `tests/VerifiedOriginalTargetTlsInteropTest.php`, real authenticated HTTPS-proxy negative interop `tests/ProxyAuthenticatedTlsInteropTest.php`, fail-closed tests `tests/VerifiedTargetTlsUpgradeGuardTest.php`. All exact-source routine workflows GREEN (PHP8.4/8.5 native, PHPForge stable/lowest QA, both analyzers, independent peers, benchmarks): [Security #40455457270](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455457270); [Benchmarks #40455456942](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456942); [Source Audit #40455456949](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456949); [Sphinx #40455456992](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456992); [Independent HTTP2 #40455456939](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456939); [Release Candidate #40455456938](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456938); [Portable Native #40455456940](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456940); [Hosted #40455456972](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456972); [Swoole #40455456937](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40455456937). HTTPS-proxy→HTTPS-origin nested TLS remains deliberately unsupported, aborts the proxy socket, and is excluded from the 3.0 supported contract; no positive nested TLS is claimed. Real TLS-within-TLS remains intentionally unsupported and is not represented as successful interoperability.
- [x] **U10.07** Implement bounded bidirectional TCP/Unix stream relay. **Acceptance:** Large hash-valid transfer with backpressure. **Proof:** Two-way SHA-valid real socket relay and 1-MiB slow-peer transfer with a bounded 65,536-byte outgoing queue; cancellation/leak proof in tests/StreamRelayTest.php, StreamRelayLargeTransferTest.php, StreamRelayLifecycleTest.php. PHPForge four lanes and analyzers PASSED at 3426f3c [#40103162591](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40103162591). U10.08 half-close still OPEN.
- [x] **U10.08** Support half-close, cancellation, timeout and worker-drain cleanup. **Acceptance:** No deadlock or orphan relay task. **Proof:** src/Network/Connection.php plus ConnectionLifecycleOperationsTrait.php and StreamRelay.php propagate cleartext TCP/Unix peer FIN only after accepted writes drain while the reverse direction stays live. Real tests/StreamRelayHalfCloseTest.php confirms request-FIN followed by complete response, WORKER_SHUTDOWN cancellation, zero watchers and subscriptions; tests/StreamRelayLifecycleTest.php covers host cancellation and monotonic deadlines. All PHPForge and static QA pass [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104) at [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793). Raw TLS FIN is not presented as TLS close_notify.
- [x] **U10.09** Measure pool hit rate, p99, CPU, FD and RSS versus direct dial. **Acceptance:** Useful gains evidenced under saturation. **Proof:** [8a62ac31](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/8a62ac3111edfba7c2fd32b3b29f55aedbbda5cd) all nine exact-source workflows GREEN: [Security #40362606052](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362606052), [Benchmarks #40362605349](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362605349), [H2 #40362604757](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604757), [Hosted #40362604732](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604732), [Portable #40362604774](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604774), [Swoole #40362604769](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604769), [Source audit #40362604827](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604827), [RST #40362604857](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604857), [RC #40362604726](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40362604726). ABBA paired real independent persistent TCP peer; two 160-request trials per mode per PHP version with 16 concurrent requests and exact 320 successful responses per mode. Pool 288/320 real reused socket hits (90%), 32 fresh pooled dials vs 320 direct, FD peak 26; captured RSS. PHP8.4 pooled/direct RPS 8686.179/4222.680 (2.057x), p99 4.211/5.341ms, CPU 18.109/35.691ms. PHP8.5 pooled/direct RPS 5094.577/4076.459 (1.2498x), p99 4.945/4.038ms (22.5% regression disclosed), CPU 31.412/39.148ms. This is developmental measured useful gain, NOT release certification.

**Batch tracker:** 9/9 accepted; 0 open — U10 DONE. Accepted U10.01–U10.09 inclusive. [C2 handoff](evidence/runwire-3.0/c2-proxy-pooling-handoff.rst); immutable nine-green QA on 7d71b589. Nested TLS through an HTTPS proxy is excluded and explicitly fails closed.

### U11 — Truly asynchronous filesystem and file streaming

**Prerequisite:** U01,U02,U06. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U11.01** Define async file API for open, read, write, stat, rename and unlink. **Acceptance:** Portable contract and errors. **Proof:** Portable async open/read/write/stat/rename/unlink/cancel signatures, mode and cancellation/deadline/error contracts in src/Filesystem/AsyncFileOperationsInterface.php, AsyncFileHandleInterface.php and Enum/FileAccessMode.php; tested in tests/AsyncFileApiContractTest.php; PHPForge four QA lanes and both analyzers PASSED at 3426f3c [#40103162591](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40103162591). Contract accepted only: real open-handle backend NOT implemented.
- [x] **U11.02** Implement bounded executor backend for blocking filesystem calls. **Acceptance:** Slow disk operation never freezes event loop. **Proof:** AsyncFileSystem uses the bounded off-loop ProcessExecutor worker queue, nonblocking reactor IPC and exact-once callback completion; tests/AsyncFileSystemTest.php proves loop heartbeat while a 60ms worker operation is busy and resource watchers return to zero. Real worker/QA at immutable 96979be [Security #40068643399](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40068643399) PASS; no kernel async or sandbox claim.
- [ ] **U11.03** Use native accelerated backend only when available and proven. **Acceptance:** No mandatory extension or fake async. **Proof:** PENDING.
- [x] **U11.04** Implement partial/offest writes and large binary file reads. **Acceptance:** Correct hash and offset semantics. **Proof:** Real worker absolute-offset writes loop on partial fwrite; bounded binary 7-byte offset fixture and exact full 1 MiB NUL/0xFF worker write/read compare SHA-256, byte length and zero worker leak. Fixed 1.6 MiB IPC packet limit preserved; all PHPForge 8.4/8.5 QA and PHPStan/Psalm PASS [Security #40068643399](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40068643399) at 96979be.
- [x] **U11.05** Stream file to/from network with low-memory backpressure. **Acceptance:** Slow network peer cannot buffer entire file. **Proof:** ScopedFileTransfer uses 16 KiB file-to-network and network-to-file slices with 64 MiB call limit on one AsyncConnection and one off-loop executor; tests/ScopedFileTransferTest.php proves 128 KiB/2 MiB real Unix socket SHA parity, delayed slow peer and positive backpressure count, live timer heartbeat and zero worker/watchers; all four PHPForge QA and analyzers PASS [Security #40068643399](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40068643399) on 96979be. Atomic filesystem and HTTP transport guarantees not claimed.
- [x] **U11.06** Implement cancellation and deadlines for queued/active file jobs. **Acceptance:** Exactly-once terminal file result. **Proof:** `AsyncFileSystem::read/write` propagate caller CancellationSource and inherited RequestDeadline to bounded `ProcessExecutor`; queued cancellations settle once with unsubscribed owners. The real Unix FIFO test `tests/AsyncFileSystemTest.php` starts a blocked active worker read and explicitly cancels it, expecting exactly one `CancelledException` completion, no active worker and zero reactor watchers/timers; `tests/ProcessExecutorTest.php` validates deadline/queued/active job retirement. Source 439408e [Security & Standards #40070492152](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40070492152): all four PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge Pest/Pint/PHPCS and both PHPStan/Psalm analysis jobs PASS, with no skipped tests.
- [x] **U11.07** Close file handles on scope, request and worker shutdown. **Acceptance:** No FD leak after cancellation. **Proof:** `tools/executor-worker.php` opens short-lived write handles and closes them in `finally`, and worker-local read handles are automatically closed after operation; `ScopedFiles::await()` cancels abandoned jobs on scope unwind. `ProcessExecutor::retire()/close()` terminates active blocked jobs and releases process pipes/read/write watchers; real blocked-FIFO cancellation and file-worker teardown tests verify 0 workers/watchers/timers after cancellation in PHP8.4/8.5 four green QA jobs [#40070492152](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40070492152) at source 439408e. Real PHP8.4/8.5 FastCGI and persistent RoadRunner per-request temporary file and socket descriptors were closed and cleaned in [Hosted #40070491774](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40070491774). No future cross-request shared file-descriptor API is certified.
- [x] **U11.08** Define atomic replace/fsync guarantees and OS limitations. **Acceptance:** Crash-safety assumptions documented and tested. **Proof:** The RST `docs/async-file-io-3.0.rst` explicitly limits worker writes to `fflush` and ordinary OS `rename`; it makes **no** fsync, parent-directory sync, cross-device atomicity or power-failure durability guarantee, and lists the complete unsupported durable replacement sequence. The worker fixture `tests/AsyncFileSystemTest.php` verifies actual binary same-directory rename replacing an old target and fails closed when the new target parent directory does not exist, without fallback copy/unlink. Exact c2ce077 strict [Sphinx #40071567309](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40071567309) and all four [Pest/analysis lanes #40071567489](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40071567489) green. This certifies **documented/tested limits**, not an implemented crash-durable replace API.
- [x] **U11.09** Check symlink traversal, permissions and path policy. **Acceptance:** Hostile filesystem operations rejected. **Proof:** Opt-in rootDirectory in src/Filesystem/AsyncFileSystem.php passes policy only to off-reactor tools/executor-worker.php; worker rejects noncanonical/out-of-root paths, dot-dot, symlink components, missing parents and UNIX unreadable/unwritable mode bits. tests/AsyncFilePathPolicyTest.php proves seven hostile permission/traversal/symlink/outside/rename/unlink scenarios, unchanged protected content and no remaining workers/watchers; PHP8.4/8.5 QA passes [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104) at [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793). Explicit limitation: lexical/path checks are NOT race-free against hostile concurrent swaps (U14.06 remains OPEN).
- [x] **U11.10** Benchmark disk throughput, loop ticks, worker load and memory. **Acceptance:** Measured improvement with bounded overhead. **Proof:** immutable [source 4c77c07](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/4c77c07d0208f6da929d1fdf606de68a3540bc71); independent [C3 five-pair worker profile #40264822586](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40264822586) PHP8.4/8.5 GREEN, 5 alternating trials ×8 verified samples at 64 ×65536 B byte-identical write/read/unlink; two-worker throughput +8.44% PHP8.4 and +10.53% PHP8.5 with CV <2.5%; actual sampled whole-tree RSS max 202.4/203.5 MB versus 384 MiB gate and sampled event-loop tick gap max 7.24/10.52 ms versus 50ms gate, with bounded workers and queues. [Security & Standards #40264827446](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40264827446) PHPForge and native PHP8.4/8.5 PASS; [Benchmarks #40264827165](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40264827165) and all seven other exact-head source workflows PASS, plus real concurrent file/socket callback [regression](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/blob/4c77c07d0208f6da929d1fdf606de68a3540bc71/tests/AsyncFilesystemSocketFairnessTest.php). This is a scoped U11 file-worker improvement, not 1800-second release soak or cross-protocol RPM certification.

### U14 — Security, fuzzing and adversarial resilience

**Prerequisite:** U03-U13. **Batch QA:** targeted unit, fault and real endpoint tests; PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest PHPForge QA where relevant; CI evidence; tracker sync before proceeding.

- [x] **U14.01** Apply outbound SSRF policy to DNS results and connected address. **Acceptance:** No address swap or rebinding bypass. **Proof:** Optional public-only OutboundAddressPolicy validates numeric DNS A/AAAA candidates before Dialer opens TCP and checks stream_socket_get_name actual peer equals approved candidate before TLS or ownership handoff; HappyEyeballs propagates policy to each candidate. Negative private/loopback/mapped, changed-peer and blocked-direct/raced fixtures in tests/OutboundAddressPolicyTest.php passed all four Pest and both analyses [Security #40073762272](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40073762272) at 8ea14d1. No implicit protection for callers omitting the policy or for future redirects.
- [x] **U14.02** Verify TLS certificate/hostname/mTLS, ALPN and proxy identity. **Acceptance:** Negative trust-store fixtures fail closed. **Proof:** Real TlsClientDialerTest exercises validated CA roots, hostname mismatch, untrusted self-signed rejection, TLS1.2/1.3 and explicit ALPN, client certificate mTLS success and required-client-identity rejection, stalled handshake cleanup and Unix/TLS downgrade rejection. TlsClientOptions never disables verify_peer or verify_peer_name. PoolIsolationKeyTest rejects mixed original TLS peer/trust/ALPN and proxy endpoint/auth policy revisions to prevent identity confusion before reuse. All PHPForge QA and both analyzers passed [Security #40072263291](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40072263291) at 00291e1. This accepts trust/identity policy negatives only; actual CONNECT/SOCKS5 tunneling and nested proxied TLS remain U10.04–U10.06 OPEN.
- [x] **U14.03** Fuzz HTTP1/H2/H3 and HPACK/QPACK/QUIC protocol state. **Acceptance:** Malformed frames preserve sibling sessions. **Proof:** tests/AdversarialProtocolCorpusTest.php validates 128 oversized H2 frame declarations, 128 H3 declared-body overflows, 32 invalid HPACK entries, 32 hostile H1 CL/TE messages, 64 oversized QPACK field sections and 64 malformed QUIC native-worker IPC envelopes, each keeping an independent valid peer decoder working. Existing Http2AbuseTest.php/Http3AbuseFaultAcceptanceTest.php cover connection-fatal/stream-local protocol state. Bounded parsers contain malformed inputs without sibling poisoning; all four PHPForge QA and both analyzers PASSED on exact d78f0f6 [Security #40110391859](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40110391859) (PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest QA and both analyzers PASS), [Sphinx #40110391510](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40110391510), [Real Hosted #40110391536](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40110391536), [Portable #40110391499](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40110391499) and [Swoole #40110391595](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40110391595). Scope excludes incomplete H2/H3 outbound clients, WebTransport and release-throughput certification.
- [x] **U14.04** Fuzz WebSocket fragmentation, invalid control frames, slow peers and session cancellation. **Acceptance:** Hard message/session budgets remain enforced under hostile traffic. **Proof:** Immutable source `3e5b96b` passed all nine PR workflows, including [Security & Standards #40457097152](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40457097152). `tests/WebSocketDeterministicFuzzTest.php` exercises 512 fragmented valid frames and 256 malformed mask/opcode/control/RSV/length frames with bounded memory and fully released `ByteBudget`. `tests/WebSocketHostileSessionAcceptanceTest.php` exercises 64 simultaneous real socket-backed sessions with fragmented interleaving, invalid control frames, oversized messages and incomplete canceled fragments; it proves exactly-once close observers, zero byte-budget retention and zero terminal loop watchers/timers. `tests/WebSocketBackpressureAcceptanceTest.php` proves real stalled-peer bounded write pressure and resumed draining; `tests/WebSocketAdversarialFrameTest.php` checks length boundaries, masking, UTF-8 and close codes. Scoped WebSocket-only acceptance; no WebTransport claim.
- [x] **U14.05** Test proxy credentials and redirection/pool isolation. **Acceptance:** No cross-origin secret exposure. **Proof:** No proxy credential exposure is permitted by the direct-origin Http1ClientRequestWriter: tests/Http1ClientRequestWriterTest.php rejects Proxy-Authorization/Proxy-Connection before emitting bytes, including frame policy; ClientRedirectRetryPolicy strips proxy-auth plus Authorization/Cookie/private headers across changed origin, with real independent dual-peer tests/Http1PolicyClientTest.php and pure policy tests. tests/PoolIsolationKeyTest.php verifies proxy route and rotated proxy-auth policy revisions cannot share tenant/origin identities. PHPForge four QA and analyzers PASS [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104) on exact [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793). No working authenticated CONNECT/SOCKS connector is claimed.
- [x] **U14.06** Audit async filesystem symlink/TOCTOU and sandbox access. **Acceptance:** Path/permission attack suite green. **Proof:** immutable W96 `4ac8cf922cb8` [PHPForge full QA/analysis #40163232571](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40163232571) PASS executes `tests/AsyncFileSystemSecurityTest.php` against actual worker with 12 path/permission outcomes: off-root, traversal, leaf/ancestor/root symlinks, denied read/write, forbidden rename/unlink/stat, authorized read, zero leaked watchers; [Sphinx docs #40163232338](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40163232338) PASS. Explicit **risk disposition**: PHP path checks remain TOCTOU-vulnerable to concurrent directory mutation; this is an **audited best-effort path boundary, NOT a race-free sandbox**. All four PHPForge QA and PHP8.4/8.5 analyzers PASS [Security & Standards #40107078104](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40107078104) at [3e31268](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/commit/3e31268cf9112c8c809936ee4dad2e6d844a5793). This is bounded-admission regression evidence, not a substitute for U15 full 180-second performance certification.

## Sphinx / Read the Docs complete feature documentation index

All of these pages are **required deliverables** inside the proposed docs documentation tree, not existing published documentation.

| Feature division | Docs page hierarchy | Mandatory content |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Installation/requirements | getting-started/, reference/capabilities/ | PHP, OS, extension, host and fallback matrix |
| All public APIs and errors | api/, reference/errors/ | Generated PHP source signatures and typed errors |
| Breaking migration | migration/2.1-to-3.0.rst | Before/after API, removal, behavior changes |
| Loops, Fiber and scope | concurrency/ | Task, watcher, cancellation, lifetime, limits |
| TCP/TLS/Unix/UDP/QUIC | network/ | Bidirectional examples, deadlines and security |
| DNS and dialer | network/dialer.rst, network/dns.rst | Racing, IPv4/6, connect and failures |
| HTTP/1.1, 2, 3 | protocols/http/ | Full client/server streaming, headers and flow control |
| WebSocket | protocols-3.0.rst | Masking, frames, sessions and callback ownership |
| Pools/proxies/relay | network/pool.rst, network/proxy.rst, network/relay.rst | CONNECT/SOCKS5, credentials, backpressure |
| Filesystem and executor | io/filesystem.rst | Genuine async, quotas, cancellation, platform limits |
| Runtime and autoscaling | runtime/, deployment/ | Native prefork/portable and hosted runtimes |
| Security and performance | security/, operations/ | TLS, SSRF, observability, p99/RSS, threat models |
| Guides/tutorials/recipes | tutorials/, examples/, troubleshooting/ | Runnable real application examples |

**Docs technical gate:** docs/conf.py, docs/index.rst, RST-only toctrees and pages, conversion of existing user guides, pinned doc requirements, .readthedocs.yaml v2, strict reStructuredText-only (.rst) Sphinx source pages, API extraction from PHP, Sphinx warning-as-error HTML build with strict refs, link checks, runnable snippets, hosted Read the Docs preview, versioned navigation and **100% shipped feature documentation coverage**. Update documentation with each implemented feature batch; U16 verifies full completeness.

## Required QA matrix and final release rules

| Gate | Requirement |
| --- | --- |
| PHP | PHP 8.4/8.5, Composer stable/lowest QA, PHPStan/Psalm, static security |
| Event loops | Portable Select, ext-event and compatible host-owned reactors |
| Hosts | Native prefork/portable, real FPM/FrankenPHP/RoadRunner/Swoole/OpenSwoole |
| Transport | Independent client/server TCP/TLS/Unix/UDP/QUIC, IPv4/IPv6 |
| Protocol | Independent H1/H2/H3/WS client and server, malformed and cancel storms (WebTransport excluded) |
| Async work | Actual nonblocking DNS and filesystem, executor quota/stop/error coverage |
| Worker lifecycle | Crash/backoff, hot reload, recycle, autoscale and active stop |
| Performance | 2.1.2 matched baseline + CV, p99/RSS, 1800-second process-tree/GC/OPcache/FD soak |
| Docs | Read the Docs Sphinx preview, full feature/API reference, strict build, runnable examples |
| Release | No unresolved blocker, complete migration, QA pinned to immutable frozen SHA |

**Historical QA transparency:** previous seven standard workflows passed on 2.2 code 8b125bab. The separate [Release Certification #39891900634](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/39891900634) **FAILED overall** due to above-ceiling benchmark variance; its individual 30-minute soak and other four jobs passed. This is not 3.0 certification. Earlier H1 passing certificates are likewise historical.
