**U06 development checkpoint (2026-10-24; pending QA, no task accepted):** The Fiber `AsyncDialer` now combines a caller-supplied cancellation token with the containing coroutine scope rather than silently discarding per-call cancellation. A completed socket is aborted when a racing Fiber cancellation prevents ownership handoff. Tests cover cancellation before launch and during a queued DNS operation, including cleanup of caller subscriptions, pending attempts and loop watches. U06.01–U06.08 remain OPEN until each full acceptance criterion and CI evidence is verified. U04.08 remains OPEN: the opt-in matched TLS workflow must be dispatched separately; ordinary successful PR benchmark jobs do not certify it.

**U04.06 hardening follow-up (2026-10-24; QA pending):** The coroutine network adapter must not turn the core `TLS_UNVERIFIED_EOF` close reason into a successful empty receive. The pending receive now rejects and reads after a truncated close report an explicit error; previously received bytes remain available to the waiting reader. Live socket-pair tests cover pending receive and fragment-then-truncation semantics. No additional canonical task is accepted by this follow-up; the U04.08 matched-TLS certification gate remains OPEN.

**U06.01 scoped-resource lifecycle checkpoint (2026-10-24; pending CI acceptance):** The coroutine scope now maintains explicit, removable shutdown callbacks for owned resources; the Fiber-facing connection adapter registers its established stream for closure on normal scope termination, unregisters after actual connection closure or intentional adapter disposal, and preserves scope-owned cancellation. The outbound adapter performs an additional cancellation checkpoint before transferring a successful dial result to a Fiber to reject a per-call cancellation race. Real socket-pair and TCP tests verify normal-exit cleanup without persistent watchers. U06.01 remains OPEN pending complete fail-fast pending-dial acceptance, full PHPForge QA and immutable CI evidence. U04.08 remains OPEN pending manually initiated matched baseline TLS certification.

**QA feedback incorporated (2026-10-24):** [Security & Standards #40046147643](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40046147643) FAILED across four PHPForge stable/lowest Pest jobs with `IC_TEST_TASK_TIMEOUT` at 300 seconds, after earlier `AsyncConnectionTest` cases passed. Two added `AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest` cancellation fixtures created a persistent executor worker before starting `CoroutineRuntime::run()` but only called `ProcessExecutor::close()` after that loop exited, preventing event-loop quiescence. The next slice closes the worker inside the event-loop cancellation callback. This is a diagnosed test-harness lifecycle defect, not evidence of successful QA. Immutable commit and fresh six-lane QA must pass before checking U06.01; benchmark matrix from the previous head is assessed separately.

**U06 scheduler-liveness next slice (2026-10-24; prepared but unaccepted):** Standalone `CoroutineRuntime::run()/runRequest()` must finish driving once its structured root has settled, rather than waiting for unrelated persistent executor FD watchers to disappear. The proposed root-task completion stop hook leaves `attachRequest()` externally driven behavior untouched, with a real subprocess executor idle-watcher regression. This is not accepted without its own PHP8.4/8.5 full QA.

**2026-10-24 rolling QA response:** PR head `63bcc1d` PHPForge matrix completed all Pest suites successfully on 8.4/8.5 stable/lowest; the four lanes failed only on Pint's PER ordering/placement rules in `CoroutineScope` and `AsyncConnection`. The next combined commit sorts those class elements and adds standalone root-stop ownership with a real persistent-executor watcher test. No tracked milestone is marked accepted pending fresh green QA and remaining independent acceptance criteria.

**U06.04 future-settlement isolation checkpoint (2026-10-24; prepared, QA pending):** An exception from one future completion observer must not prevent the remaining suspended task listeners from receiving their terminal notification. `FutureState` now isolates observer exceptions after committing resolve/reject, and a regression covers both terminal paths, preserved results/error identity and rejection of a second settlement. This is a U06.04 hardening slice, **not** a full task acceptance until cancellation-race/endurance evidence is green.

**QA remediation at 5b4dacd (2026-10-24):** The full PHPForge matrix found a deterministic request soak regression: `tests/BatchPSoakTest.php:200` showed `deferredBacklog=1` after repeated standalone root completion, because immediate `LoopInterface::stop()` during `FiberScheduler::drain()` interrupted existing deferred callbacks. Root completion now schedules its stop on the next loop turn, preserving draining and still avoiding independently owned persistent executor waiters. PHPCS also reported an unused `$scope` parameter in the new regression; the test now reads the token. Pest, PHPCS, full matrix, and non-duplicative benchmark verification remain pending on the next head. No task marked accepted based on failed QA.

**U06 rolling QA correction (2026-10-24):** The standalone root-stop experiment introduced regressions in real Swoole/OpenSwoole event loops, request reuse, Sphinx benchmarks, and structured deadlines on commit `05e2654` (Swoole workflow #40047852177; PHPForge and benchmarks failed). Removing the speculative `CoroutineRuntime::execute` stop hooks and their external-worker regression, restoring the well-tested driver. The independently implemented `FutureState` observer-isolation + Fiber wakeup test stays. Persistent externally owned watcher quiescence is deferred pending an actual cross-loop implementation and acceptance tests; U06 remains OPEN and no acceptance count increases.

**U06.03 explicit ownership transfer slice (2026-10-24; pending full QA):** Established stream `transferOwner()` replaces an existing cancellable owner only after checking the target token; the original cancellation observer and timeout are released. The coroutine `AsyncConnection::transferTo()` rejects active waits/buffered EOF, transfers to an independent token, unregisters scope-shutdown cleanup and permanently detaches coroutine callbacks, returning the core connection for worker/pool management. Regressions verify old-owner cancellation cannot close the handed-off stream, new-owner cancellation does, and failed transfer to an already-cancelled token preserves original ownership. U06.03 is OPEN pending real pool reuse and full CI acceptance.

**U06.02 follow-on deadline fault test (2026-10-24; pending QA):** A queued hostname DNS attempt inherits a 20ms nested scope deadline while an independent process worker is deliberately busy. A 60ms external-worker shutdown timer provides deterministic standalone-loop quiescence, and the test requires deadline-classified failure, zero pending races/tasks, and zero timers, stream watchers, or deferred callbacks. This covers DNS/task/deadline nesting only; U06.02 remains OPEN for TLS, HTTP protocols and files acceptance.

**U06.06 outbound admission quota checkpoint (2026-10-24; unaccepted):** A real hostname dial coordinator with `maxPending=1` must reject a second DNS race under worker congestion, retain the first owned request, and release all pending attempts, cancellation callbacks and loop handles after explicit cancellation. This complements existing task/waiter bounds; filesystem and multi-protocol queue ceilings still need full cross-feature certification. Not marked complete until QA green.

**U06.03 leak-free repeated transfer correction (2026-10-24; QA pending):** Ownership lifetime cleanup now occurs once during core `Connection::finalize`, rather than registering a fresh `onClose` observer for every token handoff. The owner binder handles already-cancelled tokens and synchronous aborts before the binding has been assigned; a regression performs 64 consecutive owner transfers and verifies the close callback registry does not grow, that every old observer is released, and that only the current owner can terminate the stream. Existing close/deadline tests remain part of the complete QA gates. No task is accepted until green CI.

**U06.07 shared callback/coroutine transport handoff (2026-10-24; pending QA):** A pooled connection, handed off by `AsyncConnection::transferTo()` from a finished structured scope, is exercised through the existing core `Connection::onData` callback using live socket data. No new socket/selector engine is introduced; this proves coroutine-to-callback reattachment and the new owner token's terminal cleanup. Other callback/coroutine protocol combinations remain OPEN.

**U06.04 deterministic terminal-race matrix (2026-10-24; pending CI):** 64 actual Fiber suspensions alternate the order of task cancellation and a future resolving, prove the child terminates exactly once with cancellation, the independently resolved future keeps its original result and rejects a second settlement, and the loop retains zero pending callbacks, timers or socket watchers. This supplements existing cancellation/future tests, but does not mark U06.04 complete until the exact commit's QA passes.

**U06.05 mixed primitive preservation regression (2026-10-24; pending CI):** One live structured scope coordinates a two-party barrier, mutex, bounded channel and snapshot task-local key, asserting generation ordering, successful message transfer and parent/child task-local isolation. Complements the 64-iteration terminal-cancellation race matrix; U06.05 remains OPEN until the new head passes QA and all required semantics are shown.

**U06.01 parent-failure pending-DNS cancellation (2026-10-24; QA pending):** Added a real child Fiber that begins queued hostname dialing through the shared off-loop resolver, followed by an intentional parent scope failure. The test requires child task cancellation/drain, zero pending logical/socket dials, no active tasks and no leftover loop watches/timers/deferred entries. An independent scheduled executor shutdown permits a standalone test loop to terminate without misattributing external worker liveness to the scope. Complements U06.01 ownership cleanup; remains OPEN pending full QA.

**QA response to 8ac3937 (2026-10-24):** All four PHPForge PHP8.4/8.5 stable/lowest QA lanes, all four Swoole/OpenSwoole lanes and both short core benchmark jobs passed. PHPStan on both PHP versions reported one narrow-path false-positive at `ConnectionLifecycleOperationsTrait.php:26`: the entry guard establishes OPEN state, but the immediately following owner attachment can synchronously close the stream when a token is already cancelled. The cleanup guard now reads `state()` afresh after the potentially reentrant attachment. Psalm passed. The exact-next-head full static suite must go green before any task acceptance; matched U04 TLS and optional long diagnostics remain distinct.

**U06.06 bounded channel-waiter admission (2026-10-24; pending QA):** A runtime with `maxWaitersPerPrimitive=1` lets the first Fiber suspend in a rendezvous channel, rejects a second receiver with `CoroutineOverflowException`, then delivers a value to and drains the original accepted waiter with no leftover tasks, timers or deferred backlog. Complements the already checked executor and hostname-race quotas, but U06.06 is OPEN pending multi-primitive and filesystem queue certification.

**H2 sustained diagnostic (2026-10-24; OPEN):** [Benchmarks #40048981478](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40048981478) failed the independent real `h2-flow-window` pressure run at `H2 phase did not drain: active=1, completed=32, remaining_bytes=10160`; the shorter core H1/H2 protocol benchmarks passed. No H2 pressure acceptance or release benchmark certification is claimed. A follow-up preserves all existing correctness/time limits and records per-stream received/expected bytes, last progress, outstanding streams and client receive window on a drain failure to isolate the H2 server/flow-control stall. Diagnose and fix the underlying transport if reproduced; do not suppress the gate or substitute a skip.

**U06.08 repeat-request mixed I/O endurance checkpoint (2026-10-24; QA pending):** Added a 48-generation live UNIX socket-pair regression, alternating event-loop deferred peer writes and Fiber timer suspension under one reused `CoroutineRuntime`. Each generation must receive its unique response, close its owned connection exactly once on scope exit, and leave zero active tasks, read/write watchers, timers or deferred callbacks. This is repeat-generation correctness evidence, not the required sustained FD/RSS and p99 scheduler-lag certification; U06.08 remains OPEN.

**U06.03 close-observer lifetime hardening (2026-10-24; pending acceptance):** `Connection::registerCloseObserver()` returns a removable handle while retaining the `onClose()` fluent interface. The core still dispatches a stable snapshot of listeners on termination; `AsyncConnection` unregisters its close observer on disposal and successful transfer to a distinct owner, preventing old request closures from accumulating on long-lived pooled connections. Regression coverage verifies selective unsubscribe, immediate post-close delivery, and removal after coroutine-to-pool handoff. Unchanged QoS and strict existing test suites are required before accepting.

**H2 real pressure handshake hardening (2026-10-24; QA pending):** The independent `h2_adaptive_matrix.py` client now completes the HTTP/2 SETTINGS acknowledgement exchange before measured streams begin; the constrained-flow profile waits for acknowledgements of both connection preface SETTINGS and `INITIAL_WINDOW_SIZE=16384`. This removes a response-versus-window-application race at connection start while preserving the same flow limits, offered load, phase durations and failure timeout. The pressure benchmark must pass for certification; instrumentation and handshake changes alone are not acceptance.

**U06.03 bidirectional connection-pool ownership (2026-10-24; QA pending):** `AsyncConnection::adopt()` now explicitly transfers a pool-owned `Connection` into a new coroutine request scope. Its constructor recognizes an already-bound matching owner token without silently replacing another owner. Failed adoption aborts the stream rather than retaining unclaimed callback/owner state. A live socket regression round-trips the same connection through twelve independent scope/pool handoffs while exchanging unique payloads and asserting that pool token subscriptions stay at one, no stale close observer remains, and request-owned callback slots do not persist. All full QA gates and transfer failure behaviors remain acceptance criteria; U06.03 is OPEN pending those results.

**QA repair for 725e573 (2026-10-24):** [Security & Standards #40049768924](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40049768924) passed all Pest, Pint, PHPCS and Deptrac suites but failed Rector in all four dependency/version lanes because `AsyncConnection::$closeObserverId` was assigned in the constructor yet also initialized inline; initialization is now constructor-only. Both PHPStan analysis lanes failed due `Connection` cognitive complexity 81 exceeding the limit 80; removable-close-observer mechanics were extracted into an internal `ConnectionCloseObserverTrait` while retaining the same `onClose`, observer subscribe/unsubscribe and stable close dispatch behavior. CI must revalidate both fixes; tracker acceptance remains unchanged until the new head's QA evidence is green.

**U06.02 filesystem deadline inheritance (2026-10-24; QA pending):** A new live one-worker `ProcessExecutor` regression deliberately backs up the worker with a longer sleep, queues a file read carrying an earlier inherited token deadline and later explicit task deadline, and requires exactly one `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED` callback, no leaked token subscriptions or queued jobs, and no loop watches/timers. This extends nested DNS scope deadline proofs to bounded off-loop filesystem work. U06.02 stays OPEN until TLS/protocol paths meet the same contract.

**U06.08 mixed-I/O regression budget extension (2026-10-24; QA pending):** The 48-generation socket/timer request test now asserts monotonic maximum event-loop lag below 1 second, bounded PHP allocator growth within 16 MiB, and on Linux no more than four additional live FDs, alongside zero per-generation socket watches, timers, deferred work and tasks. These are short real-runtime regression budgets, not the separate sustained p99 latency/RSS release certification. U06.08 remains OPEN pending targeted and full CI checks.

**Batch D1 implementation (2026-10-24; QA PENDING):** HTTP/1.1 sustained client was counting its 2s per-request timeout from *admission* into the preconnected candidate set, which can happen much earlier than its first actual writable event at 256-way concurrency. The strict 2-second timeout now starts on first write; overload and successful-request validation remain unchanged. A regression asserts that queued admission does not consume the deadline and correctness failures include the full request/error/timeout counter snapshot. Separately, mixed coroutine/socket/timer soak rises from 128 to 512 real socket generations with FD and allocator checkpoints every 64 generations, p99 response latency and loop watcher/timer cleanup requirements. U06.08 remains OPEN pending release-level sustained certification. The previous adaptive H1/H2 failure remains an active gate until full CI gives passing evidence.

**Batch D2 implementation (2026-10-24; QA PENDING):** U06.02 is being certified against the *same nested deadline* across direct and host-owned Fiber execution paths. New `AsyncDialerAndDialLimitsTest.php` coverage opens a real local TCP peer that deliberately never completes TLS; a child `CoroutineScope::withDeadline(80ms)` wraps the outbound TLS dial configured for 1s, requiring deadline-classified failure and cleanup of race, socket, worker, scheduler and loop handles. `BatchLCoroutineSchedulerTest.php` adds a request-bound native-loop `CoroutineRuntime::attachRequest()` with `RequestExecutionPolicy(maxExecutionSeconds=80ms)`, a long-sleeping child and root, and verifies cancellation of both, child-finally execution, terminal scope accounting and zero handles. Existing off-loop DNS, actual TCP socket reads and `ProcessExecutor` filesystem cases remain part of the full suite. U06.02 stays OPEN until both tests and full PHPForge QA pass on the exact new head. U06.08 and the independent H1 pressure benchmark remain separately open pending their own acceptance gates.

**Batch D3 extended endurance (2026-10-24; QA PENDING):** The mixed coroutine, real-socket, deferred-write and timer test now runs 2,048 sequential generations on the same `CoroutineRuntime`, with 32 intermediate samples. Each iteration checks unique payload delivery, terminal scope cleanup and zero scheduler/socket watchers; every 64th generation checks Linux FD drift (max +4), allocator growth (max +16 MiB) and real `/proc/self/status` VmRSS (max +32 MiB). It additionally rejects >8 MiB RSS drift between first and final sampled checkpoints, samples end-to-end p99 request latency (budget 750ms) and bounds peak scheduler lag (1s). These are real in-process evidence; U06.08 remains OPEN until exact-head full QA green, and opt-in system-level 3.0 promotion benchmarking remains a distinct final release gate under U15/U17. All acceptance counters continue to derive from checked tasks.

**U06 final acceptance (2026-10-24; source `e3fe23d`):** U06.02 and U06.08 earned task-scoped acceptance through all four PHP8.4/8.5 prefer-stable/lowest PHPForge QA jobs and both static analyzers [#40055732464](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732464), with four coroutine-host QA jobs [#40055732298](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40055732298), both H3/QUIC acceptance, source/docs/portable/hosted/release-candidate workflows. Runwire-only, no release, tag or merge.

**U07-A contract drafting (2026-10-24; QA PENDING, U07 counter stays 0/11):** Prepared transport-neutral `Http\\Client` request, upload-body, response and exchange contracts plus `ClientProtocolPolicy`, backed by five unit tests. Default negotiation demands verified TLS + exact H2 ALPN and rejects silent H1/QUIC/plaintext fallback; optional H1 downgrade requires explicit policy; H3 is eligible only when the native transport is QUIC and ALPN is `h3`. Outbound URIs reject implicit plaintext, embedded credentials, fragments, control characters and invalid host/port. The request body remains incremental, responses expose existing bounded body semantics. This is a contract-only slice: not an operational HTTP client, not U07.01 acceptance, and U05 outbound QUIC is still a prerequisite. All new files are Runwire-only; do not count progress until the code reaches PR QA and the dependency/acceptance gates are satisfied.

**Remaining U04/U05 execution groups (2026-10-24; current branch 886318d; no false acceptance):**

- **Q0 — CI recovery + fail-closed QUIC controls (implemented; pending exact-head QA):** repair six failed PHPForge/analysis lanes from [Security & Standards #40056509670](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40056509670) by removing the redundant PHPStan enum `instanceof` check in `ClientProtocolPolicy` and adding five required public API docblocks (response metadata, client request constructor and exchange method). Strengthen `QuicClientOptions` with explicit native capability inventory, rejection of loop-thread blocking handshake, stable address+port binding, no RFC 9221 datagrams and no application dispatch before verified handshake. Validate capability/negative tests; none of U05.04–U05.10 is accepted by policy-only evidence.
- **Q1 — U04.08 TLS performance certification (OPEN; external gated execution):** run existing opt-in `Benchmarks` workflow via `workflow_dispatch` with `tls_certification=true`, producing five matched alternating 180s verified TLS trials against immutable 2.1.2 and this candidate. Require comparator JSON `budget_enforced=true, passed=true`, physical CPU isolation, correct replies and archived artifacts. Ordinary green PR benchmarks are not equivalent. The existing GitHub connector currently has no workflow-dispatch action; certification cannot be truthfully marked done until a real run is triggered and passes.
- **Q2 — U05.04/05 outbound QUIC stream backend (OPEN; architectural prerequisite):** the documented native ext-quic client constructor performs a blocking TLS handshake before `setBlocking(false)`. Never call this on Runwire's event-loop thread, and never claim Fiber wrapping solves it. The workable native solution requires a persistent out-of-loop QUIC worker that owns the connection and streams, with bounded IPC, cancellation, deadline and flow-control, or a demonstrably asynchronous alternate backend; include independent real-server TLS validation and bidirectional/unidirectional stream tests.
- **Q3 — U05.06/07/08 negotiated capabilities/migration/replay safety (OPEN until transport-connected tests):** native policy explicitly rejects unimplemented unreliable datagrams, connection rebinding and early application data. Connect those guards to the eventual worker transport and verify no silently downgraded datagram, migration or 0-RTT dispatch on a real session.
- **Q4 — U05.09/10 QUIC adversity and performance (OPEN):** test independent loss/reorder/stalls/cancellation storms and collect QUIC/H3 p99, RPS, CPU/RSS and packet budget on live server. Finish U04 before accepting final U05 exit. No release, merge or tag.

**Proof discipline:** Current task totals remain **56/175** (U04 **7/8**, U05 **3/10**) until a linked run satisfies the individual acceptance statements. The QA recovery and preparatory QUIC policy features are implementation progress, not counted as completed native client tasks.

**Q2-A off-loop QUIC handshake implementation (2026-10-24; staged, QA pending):** `QuicHandshakeProbe` submits a narrowly whitelisted `quic_probe` operation through the existing bounded `ProcessExecutor`, reusing cancellation, deadlines, worker process retirement and nonblocking IPC. Only the worker constructs the native QUIC client, preventing a blocking TLS handshake on Runwire's loop. The worker rejects absent ext-quic and insecure verification options. A loopback-unreachable test verifies finite termination, exactly one callback and zero leaked process/loop resources; unsupported migration/datagram/0-RTT options fail before a job is submitted. This is a verified **handshake probe only**, not a persistent outbound QUIC session, streams or independent-server interoperability acceptance. U05.04-U05.10 remain OPEN pending a worker-owned multi-stream session backend and real server evidence. Do not mistake a passed probe for full implementation.

**QA remediation bundled with Q2-A (2026-10-24):** [Security & Standards #40057822752](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40057822752) passed the public API/Pest, Pint, PHPCS, PHPStan and Psalm gates in completed lanes but Rector rejected `void` return annotations on the two intentionally never-returning native QUIC guard methods (`assertDatagramAvailable`, `assertEventLoopSafeNativeClient`). Their return types are corrected to `never`. This fix and the new off-loop probe are submitted together for fresh full QA; neither U05 transport nor U04 matched TLS benchmark is marked accepted.

**Q2-B independent QUIC handshake interoperability gate (2026-10-24; prepared, not accepted):** The PHP 8.5 native QUIC acceptance job gains a separate independently implemented `aioquic` Python TLS 1.3 server, with ephemeral UDP listener and one-day localhost SAN certificate. A standalone PHP harness calls `QuicHandshakeProbe` through bounded worker IPC against the independent server, requires verified native handshake result and zero orphan workers, timers, read/write watches, and always cleans up the server. This verifies the isolated handshake against an independent stack, NOT reusable outbound QUIC stream ownership. The independent client connection and streams tasks U05.04/U05.05 remain OPEN until the actual persistent multiplexer, stream isolation and real reset/cancellation are proven. The CI step is opt-in via the existing H3 native PHP 8.5 job, not an additional push+PR duplicate workflow.

**Q2-C rolling QA recovery + negotiated ALPN (2026-10-24; pending new-head QA):** In [Security & Standards #40058448441](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40058448441), PHP 8.4/8.5 PHPStan failed on the worker's dynamically constructed native QUIC `close()` call and four PHPForge QA lanes failed because `echo` is forbidden in the independent handshake fixture; the H3/QUIC PHP 8.5 real interop job itself PASSED. The worker now validates that the dynamic native result is an object exposing `close()` and `getNegotiatedAlpn()`, requires the negotiated ALPN be in the verified requested ALPN list, and returns it with handshake evidence. The fixture uses `fwrite(STDOUT, ...)`, demands `h3` success, and rejects both a certificate hostname mismatch and an ALPN mismatch against the independent `aioquic` server. Added three focused `QuicHandshakeProbeTest.php` cases: malformed host/port cannot enqueue, canceled probe settles exactly once and releases watchers/subscriptions, and an earlier caller deadline cancels a queued probe before worker dispatch even if ext-quic is absent. This combined batch is implementation progress only; U05.04-U05.10 remain unchecked until persistent sessions, streams and adversarial acceptance. The opt-in matched TLS benchmark U04.08 remains OPEN, with no invented dispatch or artifacts.

**Q2-D next batch prepared (2026-10-24; code staged pending preceding full QA):** Added an internal `QuicWorkerCommand` wire format and four negative/round-trip tests as preparation for a **persistent** native-worker-owned QUIC connection and stream pool. The packet format binds a positive request correlation ID, explicit bidi/unidi stream-open/write/read/reset/close operation, opaque native stream ID, optional FIN, bounded base64 binary data (64 KiB max) and strict newline-delimited JSON; malformed/unknown commands, extra fields, arbitrary worker operations, bad stream IDs and oversized packets are rejected before IPC. This deliberately carries no PHP native object across processes. It does NOT yet establish persistent QUIC sessions or full streams; U05.04–U05.10 remain OPEN and native implementation/adversarial tests still required. Following the rolling workflow, complete CI from Q2-C before publishing this new batch or fold any Q2-C corrections together with this code.

**Q2-E actual worker-owned QUIC session implementation (2026-10-24; staged, not accepted):** Source adds an opt-in `QuicClientSession` facade owning a dedicated `ProcessExecutor(maxWorkers=1)`, retaining the same long-lived PHP worker for all commands instead of starting an incompatible second IPC backend. A worker-local `QuicWorkerSession` stores the verified native `Connection` and bounded stream object table across commands; the host sees only an epoch, monotonic request IDs and logical stream numbers. `quic_session` is a fixed whitelist operation, with validated connect/ALPN, nonblocking-after-handshake native state, bidi/unidi open, bounded writes/reads, reset and close, all using the strict `QuicWorkerCommand` envelope. Lost/invalid commands and native exceptions close the worker and invalidate the host session. Unit tests cover lifecycle, malformed/unsafe connection requests, cancellation and resource cleanup. **Still OPEN:** independent-server stream framing/echo and multi-stream fairness, invalid session recovery, fin/reset interoperability, unidirectional receive acceptance, and QUIC/H3 release benchmarks. U05.04/.05 are not checked until QA and live independent-session evidence pass; U04.08 remains a separate opt-in matched TLS certification gate. Q2-C QA must finish and failures must be incorporated into a single next push with Q2-E.

**Q2-E independent persistent-session acceptance fixture prepared (2026-10-24; QA PENDING):** The PHP 8.5 native QUIC job now runs `tests/interop/aioquic_quic_echo_server.py` using separate ALPN `runwire-echo`, a generated localhost CA/SAN certificate and ephemeral UDP port. `tests/fixtures/quic_session_echo_client.php` uses `QuicClientSession` to open **two sequential bidirectional streams on the same verified native connection**, send unique FIN-marked payloads, read actual echo bytes by nonblocking worker commands, close the native connection, and assert zero leftover Runwire watchers/timers. This is independent backend and same-session reuse evidence; initial implementation does not yet prove concurrent multiplex fairness, unidirectional peer reception, migration or datagrams. New stream-worker code remains unchecked under U05.04/05 until live CI and all PHPForge gates pass. [Prior Q2-D Security & Standards #40059182244](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40059182244) passed PHPStan/Psalm but four PHPForge lanes failed one `QuicWorkerCommandTest` invalid `toThrow(Throwable::class)` matcher and Pint's `ordered_class_elements`; both are corrected in this combined Q2-E source batch. Do not infer full release acceptance from a transport-only echo fixture.

**Q2-E worker-state negative regression expansion (2026-10-24; staged):** `tests/QuicWorkerSessionTest.php` uses a fake native object to verify binary payload write/read/FIN, worker-local stream IDs across multiple commands, explicit stream reset, fail-closed rejection of client unidirectional reads, bounded total stream admissions, stale epoch rejection and connection teardown on invalid native commands. **Q2-F real unidirectional receipt and stream-adversity batch (2026-10-24; staged pending previous QA):** Extend the independent native `aioquic` echo server to accumulate client-initiated unidirectional stream fragments up to 64 KiB, recording only a FIN-complete payload in an ephemeral receipt file. The worker-owned PHP session fixture sends a third, one-way stream after two independently echoed bidirectional streams, waits via bounded loop timers for proof of exact peer receipt, then explicitly closes the native session and asserts zero scheduler handles. Add a CI fixture path for that receipt and keep the dedicated QUIC worker fully off-loop. This tests **actual unidirectional delivery** rather than trusting `write()` alone. **Q2-E real backend partial QA and Q2-F recovery (2026-10-24):** In [Security & Standards #40059788005](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40059788005), the PHP 8.5 H3 job's **persistent outbound QUIC worker streams against aioquic echo** step PASSED (two independent bidirectional echo payloads on the same worker-owned session), and the independently verified handshake step PASSED. The full PHPForge QA remains FAILED: PHPStan flagged `QuicWorkerSession::connect()` complexity 13 (limit 12) and generic `array` vs `array<array-key,mixed>` typing; Pint flagged member ordering/blank-line policy; PHPCS flagged an unused mock `$bidirectional` parameter. These fixes are grouped with the independent unidirectional server-receipt proof in Q2-F. U05.04/05 remain OPEN until a passing exact-head full matrix and the applicable independent endpoint behavior; opt-in U04 matched TLS remains OPEN.

**Q2-F native stream partial-FIN safety (2026-10-24; QA pending):** A native QUIC stream operation carrying `fin=true` must never claim a completed send when its `write()` returns a short count; the worker now treats that as a transport error and tears down the whole session, rather than emitting a misleading success or allowing early FIN truncation. Replace external `localhost` DNS dependency with deterministic loopback IP endpoint and an explicit signed certificate SAN `IP:127.0.0.1`, preserving exact TLS host/CA/ALPN policy; match PHPForge Pint alphabetical private mutable-property order in upgrade handler. Introduce `Network\\StreamRelay`: bounded full-duplex forwarding using the existing shared `Connection` receive/send queues, explicit backpressure read pause and drain resume, per-tick forwarding limit, exclusive callback handoff and fail-closed dual abort. Independent paired Unix stream tests check simultaneous hash-correct messages, exact byte accounting and no watcher/timer leaks. Half-close, cancellation and staged shutdown remain U10.08 OPEN; full U10.07 performance/large-transfer acceptance and U09.02 WSS QA remain OPEN until exact-head verification. Canonical **100/175 (57.1%), 75 open**.

PHPForge 8.4 lowest Pest reported one WSS TLS fixture failure (812 passed): the independent TLS server callback was not entered. Include explicit sanitized fixture diagnostics (server accepted/rejected/pending TLS and client error messages) instead of guessing about certificate/DNS. Pint flagged inline closure braces in new relay; use multiline closures. W19 includes 1 MiB hash/64 KiB queue pressure test and RST feature boundaries. All unaccepted until exact-source QA; **100/175, 75 open, 32 to 75.4%**.

**W21 relay cancellation/deadline lifecycle (2026-10-24; staged, unaccepted):** Extend `Network\\StreamRelay` with optional inherited `CancellationToken` and `RequestDeadline`, register one cancellation observer and one deadline timer at `start()`, release both on all close/partial-start paths, and abort both socket owners exactly once. Real Unix stream tests verify cancellation subscriptions, deadline-driven closure, no watcher/timer leaks. Half-close and graceful one-sided draining remain explicitly unimplemented U10.08; status **100/175, 75 open**, pending exact-head QA.

**W22 exact cause of WSS and relay Pint failure (2026-10-24; QA pending):** [Security & Standards #40096500963](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40096500963) at 8378e0a passed both PHPStan/Psalm 8.4/8.5 analyses, 813 Pest tests, and PHPCS/Rector, but 1 WSS fixture failed because `stream_socket_enable_crypto()` inherited a stale **local TLS certificate path from an unrelated prior test** (`/tmp/runwire-client-tls-.../cert.pem`). Root issue: `Dialer::open()` called `stream_socket_client` without an explicit stream context, allowing PHP's process-global default TLS options to leak into unrelated outbound connections. Always create an independent `stream_context_create()` for every numeric/Unix dial before per-connection `TlsClientOptions` are applied; no trust relaxation or plaintext fallback. Pint `StreamRelay` requires a blank before `try`; the staged W21 lifecycle includes that correction. W19 1 MiB hash/pressure relay and W21 token/deadline cleanup land together in next QA. No acceptance until full exact-head green; **100/175, 75 open, target 132**.

**W23 independent default TLS-context security regression (2026-10-24; staged):** Add disposable PHP subprocess that deliberately sets a poisoned global `ssl.local_cert` path, then exercises the verified numeric `Dialer` and real `TcpListener` handshake with a trusted SAN IP certificate and exact HTTP/1.1 ALPN. Asserts TLS success despite unrelated process-global defaults without contaminating the parent Pest suite. Documents fresh per-dial contexts; prevents regression of W22 security fix. Await exact-head PHPForge before acceptance; **100/175 accepted, 75 open**.

**W24 high-density WebSocket shared-session pressure QA (2026-10-24; staged):** Extend the independent Unix peer many-session test to **128 simultaneous** shared `WebSocketSession` instances, each with fragmented masked text and interleaved ping, exact 128 deliveries, bounded per-frame/message buffers, and mandatory zero watcher/timer cleanup. Keep W10 real stalled-reader send-buffer pressure and W4 malicious frame matrix alongside this test. **W25 mTLS negative acceptance semantics after TLS context isolation (2026-10-24; QA pending):** [Security & Standards #40096884740](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40096884740) at f7113b7 passed 815 Pest tests, Pint, PHPCS and Rector on 8.4 lowest, but `TlsClientDialerTest` expected the **client** to immediately fail a TLS handshake when the **server** requires a missing client certificate. With the stale global `ssl.local_cert` leak fixed, TLS 1.3 can provisionally report client-side encryption before a server rejection alert propagates. Test the actual security guarantee: the server never activates an unauthenticated application connection (`accepted=false`); require client error when no channel was established, but permit a provisional encrypted client handle until the server rejection closes it. No change to mandatory mTLS server verification, CA validation or fail-closed policy. Combine with W23 isolated-global-context regression and W24 128-session WebSocket verification. **100/175, 75 open** pending exact-head QA.

**C1 U08.08 mixed-protocol stream/cancellation/slow-receiver stress (2026-10-25; QA PENDING):** Extend existing independent native crash/reload/manual-recycle suite to simultaneously issue 8 multiplexed H2 requests and 8 multiplexed H3 requests per wave, send one H2 RST_STREAM and one native QUIC H3 RESET_STREAM without counting canceled requests as successful, and deliberately hold H2 read/flow-control updates 20ms while accepted siblings must complete byte-identically. Repeat with HTTPS H1, TCP and UDP during live SIGKILL worker replacement, SIGHUP reload and manual generational recycle. Tighten CI JSON assertions for all five lifecycle waves, real peer streams and cancellation counts. **U08.08 remains OPEN until exact-head and native PHP8.5 real-peer QA succeed; 129/175 accepted, 46 OPEN, C1 6/11; C2-C5 queued.**


**Parallel independent C2 implementation authorized 2026-10-26 (QA PENDING):** User now allows starting independent C2 work while C1 QA runs. `BoundedConnectionPool` gains opt-in, default-off nonsecret hit/miss, fresh dial slot, waiter overflow/grant/expiry/cancel and idle-safe retention counters for eventual U10.09 matched pool/direct-dial benchmark. Tests validate real pool/socket reuse and no scope identity disclosure. **No C2 task accepted:** pool hit rates alone do not prove saturated p99/CPU/FD/RSS gains, H2/H3 multiplex slots, authenticated proxies, or WebTransport. **129/175 accepted, 46 open; C1 6/11, C2 0/10 both IN DEVELOPMENT; C3-C5 queued.** C1 acceptance gates are independent from C2 code work; no merge/release.


**C1 U08.08 acceptance + concurrent C2 development (2026-10-26):** Immutable b372832 source completed all nine workflows, specifically native independent PHP8.5 mixed 8x H2/8x H3 streams, resets, slow-receiver 20ms, live crash/reload/recycle. [Native #40218734782](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40218734782) and [artifact #12609275457](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40218734782/artifacts/12609275457) prove U08.08, with [source benchmark #40218734360](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40218734360) green; mark it accepted without claiming release U15.05. **130/175 accepted, 45 OPEN; C1 7/11 with four OPEN (U07.06/.07/.11 and U08.09); C2 independently IN DEVELOPMENT 0/10 QA-accepted; C3-C5 queued.**

**W105 PHPForge exact-head format recovery (2026-10-26; QA PENDING):** Head `927d563ef311` passed both PHPStan/Psalm analysis lanes, native QUIC PHP8.4/8.5, Pest and PHPCS, but PHPForge Pint reported exactly one `blank_line_before_statement` error in `BoundedConnectionPool::acquire()` ahead of waiter-overflow `throw` ([Security & Standards #40244499042](https://github.com/infocyph/Runwire/actions/runs/40244499042)). **W106a C2 verified outbound TLS peer provenance (2026-10-26; QA PENDING):** A client `Connection` now carries the verified peer name from the explicit `TlsClientOptions` **only after** a successful nonblocking TLS handshake and `Dialer` handoff. A synthetic plain or server-accepted connection has no such identity, and direct constructor attempts to claim verified outbound TLS without actual crypto metadata and peer fingerprint fail closed. The real trusted-CA TLS client test asserts its authenticated peer name. This provides the prerequisite for future authenticated HTTP CONNECT and SOCKS5 exchange; it does not by itself enable proxy credentials or prove nested TLS to the original target. **No task promotions:** C1 7/11; C2 0/10; 130/175 QA-accepted; 45 OPEN.


**W106b C2 guarded authenticated CONNECT/SOCKS5 exchanges (2026-10-26; exact-head QA PENDING):** In `HttpConnectTunnel` and `Socks5Tunnel`, authenticated credentials now require `verifiedProxyPeerName` matching the peer identity preserved by a successful outbound `Dialer` TLS handshake. Handshake-codec opt-in alone never authorizes credential transfer. `Socks5Tunnel` handles method → bounded RFC1929 authentication reply → CONNECT with password-method downgrade rejection and existing callback/timeout ownership. Synthetic plaintext socket tests prove no request or credentials are emitted. **Incomplete**: independent authenticated TLS peer tests, server-side connector orchestration and nested original-origin TLS; no U10 task accepted until those are verified. C1 7/11, C2 0/10, global 130/175 accepted and 45 OPEN.

The proxy tunnels now demand that internal identity for credential transmission. No synthetic accepted/unencrypted connection obtains an identity. This is an implementation correction and prerequisite to C2 authenticated-proxy conformance, not U10 accepted proof. Canonical **130/175 accepted, 45 OPEN; C1 7/11, C2 0/10**.
